### Inspection of Air Conditioner and Filling of Refrigerant
`jac_s5_en_6e6eaf00` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install Refrigerant Injector
1) Close hand valves on both sides of refrigerant oil filler
2) Filling pipe of refrigerant oil filler is installed on the connection position. Low pressure pipe is installed at low pressure pipe port, and high pressure pipe is installed at high pressure pipe port. After connection, use hands to tightening nuts.

![](images/4a8164248c3f0c1a9bd8333ea1a876496e5773edc5335df809171785c48e1c45.webp)

### 2. Discharging Operation of Refrigerant
`jac_s5_en_cf3aabcf` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Connect refrigerant oil filler to the system.
2) Place a clean cloth on the opening position of central hose.
3) Gradually open high-pressure hand valve and discharge refrigerant.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_080f7abc` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

If air exhausts is fast, refrigeration oil of compressor will discharge from the system.
4) Inspect whether there is refrigeration oil stain on the cloth. If yes, slight close hand valve.
5) When the scale of oil filler drops to 3.5kg/cm , slowly open low-pressure hand valve.
6) To decrease system pressure, it is necessary to slow open high-pressure and low-pressure hand valve until the scale shows 0kg/口(原文有误).

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_a4762b45` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

After the system discharges refrigerant, it is necessary to pump vacuum. This operation is to remove air and moisture from the system. This operation should be carried out 15mins after the installation of each part.
1) Confirm whether engine is in ―OFF‖ condition.
2) Connect oil filler to the joint of compressor and close two ends.
3) Confirm whether refrigerant is discharged from the system.
4) Connect central pipe to the air absorption position of vacuum pump.
5) After the operation of vacuum, open the high-pressure and low-pressure valve of oil filler.
6) After 10mins, scale of low-pressure tester should be more than 0.96kg/口(原文有误)(vacuum); Otherwise, it indicates that the system has leakage and should be repaired according to the following steps. Use refrigerant container to fill the system. Use leakage tester to inspect leakage position and conduct repair after any leakage is found. Discharge
refrigerant and pump vacuum from the system. If no leakage is
found, continue to pump vacuum. Re-operate vacuum pump.
8) Scales of oil filler on both ends should keep 0.96 kg/ 口 （原文有误） (vacuum).
9) Continue to pump vacuum until the scale shows 0.96kg/口 （原文有误） (vacuum)
10) After 15mins of vacuum operation, close pressure valve of oil filler and stop vacuum pump. Disconnect pipe from vacuum pump. Refrigerant can be filled.
4. Use Of Refrigerant Control Valve
1) Before connecting valve and refrigerant container, fully rotate handle to counterclockwise direction.
2) Place disc to the maximum high position counterclockwise.
3) After central pipe connects to valve device, rotate disc with hands in clockwise direction. Rotate handle to clockwise direction and drill hole on the upper of enclosed position.
5) Loosen nuts of connecting pipe connecting to central connecting port of oil filler.
6) Tightening nuts after exhaust air for several seconds.

![](images/3e215ba46cdfec8f36831c270f0cdfacc8489dd89e386d79da03737ca90bd10a.webp)

5.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_bfd42c62` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:hvac | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

This operation is to fill gas refrigerant into the system through the device on low pressure side. At this moment, place the refrigerant container straight, and gas refrigerant will flow into the system.

1) Install refrigerant container onto the adjusting valve. 2) Open low pressure valve and adjust valve to make the low pressure scale keep below 4.2kg/口(原文有误). 3) Start the engine and open the air conditioning.

![](images/be8a9479651fbd8b1ad2c094d3d53e35afc36e5610ee9b0171fa31284f53ccce.webp)

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_dae88f50` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

To prevent fluid refrigerant is filled into the compressor through inlet port, it is necessary to keep the container straight. After filling specified amount of refrigerant, close low-pressure valve.

![](images/e3c642f1a808dccfd5eac4a6a63b2eb4516348b3fdda2c3373f0727baebe1035.webp)
Close

Specified

amount of refrigerant: 625 ± 25 g

5) If refrigerant is filled slowly, put the container into the water container with temperature of about 40℃.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_c430f713` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

This operation is used when filling refrigerant through high-pressure side. Put the refrigerant container down so that refrigerant can enter into the system.
1) After pumping vacuum from the system, fully close two ends of high-pressure and low-pressure valve.
2) Install adjusting valve of refrigerant container.
3) After fully opening high-pressure valve, put the container down.
4) When system is filled too much, it will increase discharging pressure. Therefore, it is necessary to measure the weight of refrigerant while filling correct amount of refrigerant and close high-pressure valve. Specified filling amount of refrigerant: 625 ± 25 g
5) After filling the specified amount of refrigerant, close oil filler valve.
6) Use leakage tester to inspect whether there is gas leakage.

![](images/37a7613b00f44221c73cd39fab98c7b9ef997e1ab1476ecd389cf0d92a4a895a.webp)

When filling refrigerant from high-pressure side, do not start the engine. When filling fluid refrigerant, do not open low-pressure valve. Before releasing filling tester, it is necessary to conduct performance test.

![](images/e2922494e8bdb7f54f96e3e617233795534741b1a2f4e13b0259dbc38b958366.webp)

### 1. Overview of System
`jac_s5_en_f3bfee76` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:hvac | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

This model uses electric control air conditioning system. Condenser of air conditioning uses sub-cooled type, which can improve refrigeration performance. To help defrost function, when selecting mixed type or defrost mode, it automatically switches to outer circulation mode. Condenser fan (water tank fan) has low and high control stages depending on the vehicle speed, cooling water temperature and refrigerant pressure of air conditioning. The increase of air volume of blower and improvement of air mixing ability can improve the refrigerating and heating performance.

### Descriptions of Buttons:
`jac_s5_en_faf2bf12` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

A: Air speed adjusting knob. High gear has high air volume.
B: Mode key. Different mode has different function. This key is started when the indicator lights up. C: Temperature adjusting knob. When rotating in clockwise, temperature increases; while rotating in counterclockwise, temperature drops.

### Hand Operation:
`jac_s5_en_7fd106c1` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

8 (Fan): When rotating fan control button in clockwise, fan speed will increase; while rotating fan control button in counterclockwise, fan speed will decrease. From gear 1 to 4, air volume gradually increases.

(Defrost mode): this mode can guide air flow to front windshield and side window glass to defrost.

A/C (Air conditioning): when pressing this button, air conditioning compressor is either turned off or turned on. When pressing A/C button, the indicator light on the key will light up. This indicates that the air conditioning compressor is turned on and begins to refrigerate. When repressing this button, air conditioning compressor is turned off. On hot weather condition, open window to dispatch hot air out of the vehicle, and then close the window. This is helpful for shortening refrigeration time and improving working efficiency of the system.

(Internal circulation mode): this mode can stop air from entering into the vehicle. It can stop air and bad smell from entering into the vehicle or helps to rise up or drop air temperature inside vehicle quickly. When pressing the button, internal circulation mode is turned on or turned off. Long time use of internal circulation mode may cause window fog. If there is window fog, select defrost mode.

(Rear defog): When pressing this button, rear window defogger is turned on or turned off. When pressing this button, the indicator light on the key will light up. This indicates that the rear window defog function is actuated. When repressing this button, rear window defog function is disabled. If you don‘t manually turn off this function after it is running 20min, this function will be automatically disabled.

(Face air mode): this mode can guide air flow to outlet on the front of instrument panel. (Face and feet mode): this mode can guide some of air flow to the outlet on the front of instrument panel and some of air flow to the outlet of floor.

(Feet mode): this mode can guide most of air flow to the outlet of floor. It is necessary to ensure that there is no barrier under the seat to facilitate air smoothly flowing to the rear of vehicle compartment.

(Feet defrost mode): this mode can guide some of air flow to the outlet of floor and some of air flow to front windshield and side window glass for defrosting.

Defog and defrost: fog inside window is made of moisture on window glass through condensation. If temperature control system is correctly used, this condition can be greatly reduced. Defrost mode can be used for defogging and defrosting for front window glass. Defrost mode can be used for defogging and dehumidifying and provide heat for passengers. It can also quickly defog or defrost for front window.

Notes: When defogging, it is necessary to turn on the A/C switch.

Defogger for rear window and external view mirror: Rear window defogger can work only when ignition switch is switched on. Rear window defogger uses heater wire to defrost or defog for rear window. Before using this function, it is necessary to clean the snow deposited on the rear window.

### Hand Operation:
`jac_s5_en_d9485610` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Notes: it is strictly forbidden to use sharp tool to scratch, rub the rear window; otherwise, it may cut or damage heater wire. Do not attach temporary vehicle license, adhesive tape, window pattern paper or other similar objects to the defogger.

Adjustment of outlet: Use central operating rod of each outlet to change the direction of air flow.
Use the roller wheel besides side outlet to open and close outlet.

### 1. A/C Air conditioning Host Assembly
`jac_s5_en_a40cde1f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:hvac | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Air Conditioning host assembly consists of blower, filter, evaporator, expansion valve, heater and housing, etc. blower absorbs external air and blows it into the vehicle through evaporator and heater, etc. to achieve normal working of air conditioning.

![](images/c7657566fa13395928ce8ce81a2e5ba17559b3caa2d49c08f98fe6307259579a.webp)
Explosive View of A/C Air Conditioning Host Assembly

1- Filter element 2- Internal and external circulation ventilation door motor 3- Blow motor
4- Speed adjusting resistance 5- Right heater housing 6- Evaporator core 7- Mode ventilation doormotor
8- Cooling and heating ventilation door motor 9- Heater core 10- Left heater housing 11- Ventilation door mechanism 12- Middle separator 13- Lower housing of evaporator 14- Drip pipe 15- Expansion valve

### 2. Blower
`jac_s5_en_6ad4a44c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Blower consists of motor and fan. It uses air entry gate motor to switch internal and external air mode and absorbs internal air or external air. Air entry gate motor function is achieved by internal and external air button of controller. When it is in internal mode, air enters into from two inlets on both sides; when it is in external mode, air enters into from the filter on the protective cover on the front of the vehicle. At this moment, use blower to absorb air and use evaporator to conduct cooling and heating switch. The refrigerated air enters into the vehicle.

### Evaporator Temperature Sensor
`jac_s5_en_d8669d2c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Functions

It can feel the temperature of evaporator core and transmit this temperature to AC panel (FATC) to prevent freezing of evaporator.

Installation position is shown in figure.

It is installed inside evaporator core. There is thermistance with negative temperature factor inside sensor. Therefore, when temperature drops, resistance increases. When temperature increases, resistance drops.

![](images/23e4abbda6c7e5ce673c23c128441ef96a3ff56a13b2a24434b20332dae4ed4e.webp)

### Functions
`jac_s5_en_53f621dd` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

It is installed on the inlet of evaporator. It uses its release function to convert high-temperature and high-pressure fluid refrigerant flowed from condenser into low-temperature and low-pressure wet saturated and evaporation state, so as to make the evaporator easy to achieve evaporation and refrigeration functions.

开

Position

It is installed on the inlet of evaporator.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_b5c03f9a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When expansion valve cover is falling down, heat from engine will be transmitted to temperature wrap, which can cause the pressure rise of expansion valve and excessive evaporator refrigerant condition occurs. If the evaporator can‘t exert its evaporation function, fluid refrigerant will flow into the compressor and damage the compressor.

### 8. Evaporator and Heater Assembly
`jac_s5_en_a45c27a5` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The air from blower will decrease its temperature through evaporator. According to the opening degree of temperature control ventilation door, some air from evaporator is transmitted to heater core and the rest air is transmitted to the upper of heater housing. To control indoor temperature adjustment and air mixture, air from heater core and air from the upper of heater housing are mixed on the rear of heater housing. This mixed air is transmitted to the required outlet through the function of mode ventilation door motor, so as to achieve vehicle inside temperature adjustment and defrost/defog functions. This means that the heater assembly adjusts air mixing, temperature control and air direction.

![](images/5da58da33cfb9cb1ffbe3be062f920830b6d0ef4d070877497a67661cfe32d6f.webp)

9.

### Mode Ventilation Door Motor
`jac_s5_en_da106bcc` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:hvac | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

#

Functions

It is installed on the left of heater housing and controlled by signal of air conditioning panel (FATC) mode switch and starts small motor inside ventilation door motor and adjusts mode ventilation door position. When motor is started, the changeable voltage difference will be reflected to FATC and FATC will judge the feedback signal. When ventilation door reaches the required position, motor stops working.

### Functions
`jac_s5_en_50d61c08` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Its function is to cool high-temperature and high-pressure gas refrigerant from compressor under normal temperature. The cooling of condenser depends on the compulsory air volume of traveling wind and fan. Condenser of this vehicle model uses parallel flow type combined with drying bottle. The overcooling can improve refrigeration performance and reduce the refrigerant amount required by the system. It is combined with drying bottle which reduces the quantity of parts required. When the system is polluted, in traditional model, you need to replace drying bottle s, but for this vehicle model, you only need to separate the cover under the drying bottle s and then replace

![](images/53401ac5b50aa5aeb99a8a0998c18b49dfff0ed9617a80b0de57546d680a3780.webp)

desiccant.

Installation position

### Functions
`jac_s5_en_62c39617` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:lighting | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

It is installed on the dashboard inside the front windshield which can form current sensor depending on sun lighting condition. The formed current value is sent to BCM, and BCM will judge and adjust indoor temperature (automatic A/C) according to the input current value, and then headlamp will also automatically light up.

### Engine Cooling System
`jac_s5_en_0f518f79` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Concept of cooling fan

After comparing the input vehicle speed, A/C load and cooling water temperature value, engine ECU will control the rotation speed of cooling fan in two phases (high/low).

If vehicle speed sensor and cooling water temperature sensor have faults, it will drive cooling fan at high speed rotation.

Conditions for cooling water temperature controlling the turning on/ off of air conditioning compressor are shown as follows:

![](images/733ec731ecb5bba86ead272680bb823a56644d8984777aa5bc34ad0a3c3a3a76.webp)

Air conditioning compressor is turned off due to overheat the turning on temperature is 115℃.

### 14. Three-way Pressure Switch
`jac_s5_en_b2bf1230` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Functions

Three-way pressure switch refers to the switch added with a middle pressure switch based on the dual pressure switch of AC. When system pressure rises up, middle switch is started and sends signal to engine control unit. At this moment, water tank fan is in high speed status to prevent performance drop when pressure rises up. It can protect air conditioning system when pressure (high-pressure/ low-pressure) is abnormal.

Installation position

Installation position is shown in figure.

![](images/f29f4c218d734141872f8755ab3eb731be9e1eff858bde116e5ecad0100b2cd3.webp)

1. Dismantling Of Air conditioning Host Assembly

1) Dismantling and installation

Recycle refrigerant from the system by
using special refrigerant recycling device. Disconnect negative cable of battery.
Open engine compartment and disconnect heat water pipe joint.
Dismantle fixing bolts of expansion valve. Loosen bracket bolt of dashboard, and
remove dashboard module.
Dismantle dashboard. Dismantle fixing nuts and bolts of air
conditioning host assembly.
Disconnect wire harness connector and take out air conditioning host assembly.

### Functions
`jac_s5_en_479b54b8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

It uses particle type filter to remove foreign matters and unpleasant small to maintain comfortable environment.

Replacement interval

If the filter isn‘t replaced for a long time, it may cause blockage by foreign matters, blower noise increase and air volume reduction, etc.

The replacement interval is 5,000\~12,000 km. In areas with serious air pollution or bad road conditions, the inspection and replacement should be more frequent.

Replacement steps

Press two sides of sundries box and remove sundries box.

![](images/0c1ff7321418694dcf3320f49e0cd7dee8b18b6b4408829294b3c9211df9d7e5.webp)

Pull up the A/C filter cover and remove A/C filter cover.

③ Pull up the handle of A/C filter and take out A/C filter.

Installation

The installation should be in accordance with the reverse order of dismantling.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_371880fe` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When replacing filter, arrow direction on the front end towards core side. Do not install it

narrowly.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_621132d6` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

stands for inspection, adjustment, repair and replacement if necessary. When replacing tion oil, replace with new oil according to the specification of compressor.

![](images/d699d26f68983c83dc3d22cdbe2b8141cb8726e4e283f94093952f8e643037d2.webp)

### Warning:
`jac_s5_en_b39ce4e0` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:steering | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

During repairing, if correct procedures are not followed, it may cause SRS exploding during repairing and cause serious accident.
Error occurred during repairing may cause abnormal exploding when airbag is required, and cause serious accident.
Only professional repair technician is allowed to operate SRS system and its surrounding parts. Before repairing (including dismantling, installation, inspection or replacement of parts), carefully read each instruction and follow the correct procedures stated in repair manual to operate.

1. Do not dismantle some airbag parts such as airbag assembly on Driver side and passenger‘s side.

2. Do not repair any of the following parts, because these parts only can be replaced with the suitable similar replacement parts.

1) Airbag on driver side.

2) Airbag on passenger‘s side.

3) Front row airbag.

4) Seat belt tensioner.

5) Airbag control module.

6) Timing spring.

3. Do not expose airbag to high-temperature environment or near flame.

4. If airbag is stained with butter, cleaning agent, oil or water, etc., immediately use dry cloth to wipe it.

5. Do not make the airbag fall down. Do not use the fallen airbag.

6. Obtain original replacement parts only from the authorized dealer.

7. After dismantling of airbag, make it upwards and store it in stable place. Do not put any article on the top of it.

8. Verify whether displacement part number is correct. Do not use other vehicle parts as a substitute.

9. Even the airbag doesn‘t explode in slight collision accident, it is necessary to inspect cushion surface and airbag sensor. If any depression, crack or deformation is found, replace with a new safety assembly.

10. When disposing vehicle or airbag, it is necessary to first dispose airbag.

11. After airbag explodes, air charging device becomes hot, so it is necessary to treat it after it is cooling. Do not use water to drop the temperature.

12. It is strictly forbidden to measure the resistance of airbag module circuit, because this may cause airbag explosion.

13. When repairing vehicle or dismantling steering wheel, it is necessary to rotate steering wheel, so that timer spring assembly can be locked on proper position. Otherwise, it may cause timer spring assembly damage.

14. Extremely dangerous! It is not allowed to use children‘s seat when you are on front-row seat!

15. Before the product is properly installed on the vehicle, it is strictly forbidden non-professional personnel to switch it on, to avoid accidents.

16. During vehicle traveling, it is required to tie seat belt. Airbag and seat belt should be combined to use to obtain optimal protection effect during an accident. Do not make mistake to think that only airbag without seat belt is ok.

17. After turning on the power supply, if airbag indicator light constantly lights up or doesn‘t light up, it indicates that SRS system of the vehicle has fault. This fault indicates that ECU itself is damaged or ECU isn‘t switched on (or wire harness has short-circuited).

### Warning:
`jac_s5_en_8f90bf15` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

18. It is strictly forbidden to go to unauthorized repair shop for dismantling and repairing.

19. When repairing vehicle (especially using electric welding), always remember to turn off the key switch. It‘s better to completely disconnect positive pole of battery.

20. When repairing vehicle, it is necessary to use special tool to dismantle airbag assembly. After airbag explosion, airbag components, timer spring should be completely replaced. The controller (ECU) should be submitted to the authorized repair shop for treatment before being reused.

21. Airbag components should be placed in area with ambient temperature of less than 90℃, relative humidity of ≤75%, and keep away from disturbance of electric field.

22. It is strictly forbidden to let controller connect to AC current or DC current exceeding 16V, to avoid damage.

### Overview of SRS supplemental restraint system
`jac_s5_en_82808023` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Supplemental restraint system consists of airbag, auxiliary airbag, front-row airbag, timer spring, airbag module, seat belt pretensioner with explosion device on driver side, and seat belt pretensioner with explosion device on passenger‘s side. If the driver doesn‘t tie seat belt, seat belt switch will not be switch on, and SRS will light up seat belt warning light on dashboard after receiving signal. If the driver tied seat belt, seat belt warning light will go out.

When ignition switch is placed to ON position, SRS system will be started and SRS will conduct self-inspection according to the set procedure. If the indicator light lights up for 6s and then goes out, it indicates that the self-inspection passes and system is normal. After this moment, SRS will enter into monitoring status. If indicator light flashes intermittently or constantly lights up, it indicates that the self-inspection doesn‘t pass and system is abnormal. If SRS doesn‘t emit ignition command during normal traveling condition (including traveling on various road surface and disturbance), the airbag will not make function. When the vehicle has serious collision (front, side), SRS will emit ignition excitation to excite airbag components to extend airbag to absorb front and side collision energy on driver and front-row passenger‘s sides and reduce possible injury of passengers. At this moment, seat belt pretensioners on driver and passenger‘s sides are also excited, and seat belt is shrank compulsorily, which can pull the passengers left seat due to inertia back to their seat, so as to prevent collision and protect the passengers.

### 2. Principles of Restraint System
`jac_s5_en_849518a2` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When vehicle has a collision, the vehicle speed will dramatically change. Due to inertia, passengers on the vehicle continue to go forward; therefore, a collision between passenger and vehicle structural components occurs. The function of SRS is to avoid or reduce the injury caused by a collision between passenger and vehicle structural components by using restraint system (including seat, seat belt and airbag, etc).

### 1) Schematic Diagram of Functions of Restraint System
`jac_s5_en_f4ce1ae0` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/643b9d61f19f8c52ad01c7f5140e764498580bef1a1411568c1f6b02a65462c5.webp)
Schematic diagram of functions of restraint system

### 2) Principles of Airbag Functions
`jac_s5_en_8e5f20be` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Before a collision between passenger and vehicle structure components occurs, it can rapidly insert an inflated airbag between passenger and vehicle structural components and absorb passenger‘s kinetic energy through its air permeability and exhaust throttle of discharge hole, so as to relieve and separate passenger and vehicle structural components for the purpose of protecting passengers.

![](images/05fe9df0269e62ac4d724d1e0802ea53887b61a48741ab6e64fe83e5551d445d.webp)

### 3) Principle of seat belt functions
`jac_s5_en_3175edaa` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

First, it can timely tighten up and ―press‖ the passenger onto the seat by virtue of collision acceleration, vehicle inclination angle and acceleration of fiber belt. And then it is slowly loose after the impact peak passes or person is protected by airbag.

### Precautions
`jac_s5_en_85614652` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Regularly inspect whether all seat belts have abrasion or other damage. If safety system components damaged, replace them as soon as possible.

Keep the seat belt clean and dry. If the seat belt is stained, please use warm soap agent or warm water to clean it. Do not use bleaching powder, dye, strong detergent or grinding agent to clean it, because it may cause damage or weakness of fiber of seat belt.
If the vehicle has a traffic accident, it is necessary to replace complete seat belt assembly and components used, even if they are not damaged.
Do not disassemble or repair seat belt system. Maintain the seat belt and make sure the quality of seat belt hardware is good, and make sure that they are not misused or damaged by vehicle doors and seat hinges.
Do not use electric tester to inspect the seat belt pretensioner.
Do not use the disassembled buckle or seat belt assembly.
If fixing bolts are deformed or worn, please replace them immediately.
Do not lubricate pressing tone and buckle.
If parts of seat belt assembly have any problem, do not repair them, just directly replace them.

When replacing seat belt assembly, use original JAC seat belt assembly.

### Precautions:
`jac_s5_en_01b132aa` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Before dismantling or installing, please turn off ignition switch and disconnect negative pole of battery, and wait for at least 3mins.

Dismantle front doorsill protective plate and back doorsill protective plate.

Dismantle and move lower trimming cover, and unscrew lower fixing bolts. Specified tightening torque: 45N•m-55N•m

Dismantle lower trimming plate of B post.

Dismantle upper trimming plate of B post.

Unscrew upper fixing bolt and unload upper fixing anchor. Specified tightening torque: 45N•m-55N•m

Unscrew assembly bolt, disconnect connector and remove retractor.

Specified tightening torque: 45N•m-55N•m

Disconnect

pretensioner connector and insert screwdriver wrapped by adhesive tape into the cut, and then pry lock device to disconnect connector.

The assembly should be in accordance with the reverse order of dismantling.

### Seat belt pretensioner
`jac_s5_en_3d760beb` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Seat belt pretensioner can cooperate with airbag. If there is a sudden stop of the vehicle or passenger has a sharp forward inclination, seat belt retractor enters into lock status. On some degree of front collision, seat belt pretensioner takes action and pulls the passenger firmly to damp forward force of the passenger.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_84ddf15b` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When there is a collision, after the pretensioner takes action, please timely replace seat belt.

1. Working principles of seat belt pretensioner: the powder in gas generator is fired and gas pressure will be generated in pipe. A shot put in pipe is pushed and rotates around drive gear, and drive gear will drive mandrel to rotate in reverse direction. Fiber is shrank and shot put is collected by ball tank (front-row is equipped with explosive pretensioner).

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_81127234` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

If auxiliary restraint system such as ―airbag‖ and ―seat belt pretensioner‖ is used with seat belt, it helps to reduce risk of injury of driver and front-row passenger when a collision occurs. To avoid fault of airbag system and increase of risk of personnel injury and death when a vehicle collision occurs, all maintenance operation should be conducted by JAC authorized repair service center.
Improper maintenance including incorrect dismantling and installation of seat belt pretensioner may cause error action of the system and cause personnel accidents.
Unless operation stated in this manual, it is not allowed to use electric tester to test any circuit of SRS system.
3. Dismantling and installation of seat belt pretensioner
Please refer to ―dismantling of seat belt‖.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_a0930234` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Before dismantling or installing, please turn off ignition switch and disconnect negative pole of battery, and then wait for more than 3mins.
Do not use electric tester to inspect seat belt pretensioner.

The controller will calculate crash signal and compare it with set ignition doorsill valve value to determine whether it is necessary to send ignition signal and blast airbag. It is installed under the reinforced plate of B post and fixed by a bolt.

### Supplemental Restraint System Diagnostic Module (SDM)
`jac_s5_en_0424bb6f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Besides ECU, supplemental restraint system controller also includes electronic sensor, safety sensor, standby power supply, and constant voltage control circuit and fault self-diagnosis circuit.

![](images/17813fd95faa3b2f1014ece07edd0e778764f9c3c1ea078ff38e7db9a975272b.webp)
Element diagram

Electronic sensor: it converts acceleration signal of the vehicle into electric signal and then sends this electric signal to controller, and the controller will identify vehicle crash condition and determine whether airbag system ignites and when ignites.

Safety sensor: it is used to prevent error action of airbag caused during non-crash condition. It is a kind of mercury switch. When a vehicle collision occurs, it has sufficient deceleration inertia force to pull up mercury and switch on the circuit of ignition controller. In design, it is necessary to calculate the installation angle of safety sensor according to crash explosion critical acceleration.

ECU: it outputs electric signal according to electronic acceleration sensor and calculate it according to the specification, and then determines whether and when to send ignition command to actuating circuit. It also has black box function: record whether the airbag system has fault during a collision occurs. If there is a fault, it will record DTC. If there airbag system takes action, it will record acceleration and ignition time during a collision occurs. It won‘t loss its data even the power supply is switched off. It also can communicate with computer and read the stored data. If its memory is full, data will be cleared automatically.

Standby power supply: if a serious collision accident causes the situation that power supply stops supplying electric, the standby power supply of the system will continue to supply electric to ensure system reliability. After power supply is cut for 1.5s, the energy of auxiliary power supply should discharge automatically.

### 1. Function of SDM
`jac_s5_en_fe3c106c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Induce vehicle collision.
2) Ignite airbag on driver side and passenger‘s side.
3) Ignite pretensioning seat belt on driver side and passenger‘s side.
4) Monitor working condition of airbag system.
5) Report airbag system to the driver through warning indicator light: ready or has fault.
6) Diagnose and repair airbag system through series diagnosis communication port.
7) After a collision occurs, record relevant crash information data and working conditions of each
SRS part.

8) After a collision occurs, it sends crash signal to BCM module to let BCM to unlock the locked door to facilitate escaping of passengers.

### 1. Diagnosis of airbag system
`jac_s5_en_db034348` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

After turning ignition switch to ―ON‖ position, airbag indicator light located on dashboard will flash for several seconds and go out. This indicates that airbag system is in normal working condition. If airbag indicator light doesn‘t light up or constantly lights up after turning ignition switch to ―ON‖ position, it indicates that airbag system has faults. To further determine fault cause, it is necessary to use special diagnosis tester to diagnose it.

1) Use diagnosis tester to read DTC.
2) Repair it according to the instructions of DTC.
3) Use diagnosis tester to clear DTC.

### 2. DTC table
`jac_s5_en_192a39d8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/2a6a3a2ec8de893ba96d2d892bfb0a17832e2b090105857e94a7fd76fb60f080.webp)

![](images/7a65845b53ef37fd369d4a5eaceed3c56422eea09efa0945aeb778e489dccfc8.webp)

Except for operation stated in this manual, it is not allowed to use electric tester to test any circuit of SRS system. SRS circuit wire harness can be identified by yellow or orange wire harness or wire harness connectors. Do not try to repair, connect or modify SRS wire harness. If the wire harness is damaged, replace with a new wire harness. Keep the ground wire clean. X431 diagnosis tester can be used to achieve diagnosis functions and read SRS self-diagnosis result. Diagnosis mode can help repair technician to locate and inspect fault parts.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_10c4248a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

After any collision occurs, it is necessary to inspect seat belt assembly of all seats, including retractors and other metal parts.
If a front collision which can ignite airbag occurs, it is necessary to replace seat belt pretensioner even if seat belt isn‘t used.
If the following conditions occur, please replace seat belt assembly (including fixing bolt): The seat belt is being used when a collision occurs (unless the collision is very slight and seat belt, retractor and buckle are not damaged and their functions are normal).
Seat belt is damaged completely during an accident. (Which means seat belt is damaged and retractor is bent or has sliding groove).
Fixing point of seat belt is completely damaged during an accident. Before installing new seat belt assembly, it is necessary to inspect whether fixing area of seat belt is damaged or deformed. If necessary, repair it.

Fixing bolt is deformed or worn.

2. The following items should be inspected when airbag isn’t exploded during a low speed crash, turning over, and rear-end accident of the vehicle:

Inspect whether SDM housing and mounting bracket have impression, crack or deformation.

Inspect whether connector is damaged and terminal is deformed.

Inspect whether fixing bolt and ground wire are in good condition.

### 3. Repair and inspection of SRS system
`jac_s5_en_44aed1a8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:steering | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) SDM

① Inspect whether cushion cover has impression, crack or deformation.

② Inspect whether connector is damaged, terminal has deformation and wiring is sticking.

③ Inspect whether gas generator housing of airbag has impression, crack or deformation.

④ Install airbag components onto the steering wheel, and inspect its matching or alignment condition with steering wheel.

2) Timer spring

① Inspect whether timer spring connector and protective sleeve are damaged and whether terminals are deformed.

② visually inspect whether housing is damaged.

3) Steering wheel and steering post

① Inspect whether electric wiring (installed inside steering wheel) and connector are damaged and whether terminal are deformed.

② Install airbag components and inspect their matching or alignment with steering wheel.

③ Inspect whether steering wheel has noise and whether operation is flexible and end play is too excessive.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_ce6c479d` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

First center the timer spring on the bottom of steering wheel, and then install steering wheel onto steering post.

Centering method of timer spring:

First completely rotate timer spring to clockwise direction, and then rotate more than 2.5 circles to the reverse direction to make the arrows on the timer spring align with each other.

4) Other parts

Inspect whether wiring is sticking, connector is damaged and has bad connection, and whether terminals are deformed.
If there is any fault of all airbag components, they must be replaced and they should not be reused after repairing.
If it is necessary to dismantle or replace parts of SRS system due to maintenance and troubleshooting, etc., please follow operation sequences.

5) SDM

### Overview of system
`jac_s5_en_16b5daaf` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Central door lock system consists of indoor junction box, central lock relay, main control switch of electric window, two front and two back doors locking mechanism assemblies. Of which four door locking mechanism assemblies are integrated with door lock core. There is a door lock switch on left front door lock mechanism assembly. Unlocking and locking of all doors are achieved either by using main control switch of electric window, or by using lock switch on left front door lock mechanism assembly, or by using a remote controller.

### Control principles
`jac_s5_en_e399bc5f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

It uses key remote control button, main control door switch and left front door key to lock or unlock all doors. BCM control module receives unlocking or locking signal and sends this signal to indoor junction box SJB through CAN bus, and indoor junction box SJB controls central control lock relay which can turn on or turn off door lock motor. No matter which position the ignition switch is turned, central lock is in operable condition.

When the vehicle travels at the speed of higher than 40km/h and all doors are closed, BCM control module will automatically lock all doors. When a vehicle collision occurs, after receiving crash information from SDM, SJB control module will lock all doors if doors are in locking status.

Door lock motor is controlled by SJB control module. Positive and negative poles of battery are respectively connected to two terminals to make the motor rotate towards one direction. If the current direction is in reverse direction, motor will rotate also in reverse order.

When unlocking, the unlock relay on the indoor junction box SJB will actuate. Current flows to terminal of four door lock actuator through unlock relay, and then flows to unlock relay through actuator motor and terminal 4 of door lock actuator. And then current will flow out of door lock relay and achieve unlock function of door actuator through body ground wire G03.

Wiring of central lock

![](images/23a857d3d4ce7352d5f54ebb2eb68c0f0c8e1357e824096e3eb9625ee3bf51c8.webp)

### Precautions
`jac_s5_en_d206e555` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Dismantle negative pole of battery and wait for at least 1min before dismantling electric connector; otherwise, it may cause vehicle damage.
Before dismantling and installing elements, use a piece of cloth to protect parts to be dismantled to prevent damage.
When dismantling metal clips from trimming parts, use a piece of cloth to wrap the blade of slotted screwdriver.
Pay attention not to damage body elements.
When installing body trimming parts, make sure that clips are firmly installed inside plate hole on the body, and then carefully insert them into.
When dismantling some elements which can‘t be finished by one person, two persons are required to prevent parts from falling down.
When dismantling some trimming parts, do not use hard force, because it may cause part deformation.

### 1. Turning signal light and hazard warning light
`jac_s5_en_37f5e959` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When turning ignition switch to ON position, if steering switch is switched on or disconnected, relevant turning signal light will flash or go out; meanwhile tell-tail light and flash relay on dashboard will be triggered or switched on, and they will flash or produce sound with the same frequency.

2) When turning the turning signal light to left or right side, BCM will drive relevant turning signal light to flash at least 3 times.

3) If left turning signal light is turned off after being actuated, it will stop immediately after finishing initial 3 times minimum flashing cycle.

4) If left turning signal light is actuated while turning light switch is pushed from left to right side, the left turning signal light will stop immediately and right turning signal light will be turned on immediately.

5) If right turning signal light is actuated while turning light switch is pushed from right to left side, the left turning signal light will be turned on immediately.

6) Hazard warning light consists of all turning signal lights. These turning signal lights can be driven to send warning even the key is in any position. When pressing next warning light button, warning light begins to flash, and then pressing warning light button again will make the warning light stop.

7) When ignition switch is turned to ON position, the operation priority of turning signal lights and warning lights is specified below: if the turning signal light is in working status, turn on warning light switch, the warning light will substitute turning signal light to work, and the system can return to turning signal light working condition until warning light switch is turned off.

8) If a crash signal is received, all lamps will work for at least 5s under warning light working status. After 5s, press warning light switch again to turn off warning light flashing action. This is to prevent warning light being pressed due to crash.

9) When turning signal switch reports fault due to short-circuit, the short-circuit condition will be recorded into memory through relevant DTC. If immediately turn off high drive chip of turning signal light on this side, turning indicator light on combination meter will also stop. When turning ignition key from ON to OFF/ACC and then turning it from OFF/ACC to ON, the output recovers.

10) When working in turning signal light working mode and turning signal light one some side reports fault due to open-circuit, the open-circuit status will be recorded into memory through relevant DTC and turning signal light on this side will flash with double times of frequency, and turning indicator light on combination meter will also flash with double times of frequency. When ignition key is turned from ON to OFF/ACC position and then from OFF/ACC to ON position, output recovers to normal condition. Or in this ignition cycle, when open-circuit fault is eliminated, flashing with normal frequency can also be recovered.

![](images/e007da6f0e4ed3a3641792c4eb3c82fefd59c8e9e628dcff137877201c62c897.webp)

### 1. Turning signal light and hazard warning light
`jac_s5_en_6c2b1405` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Remark: circle section means the warning light signal can be turned off when crash signal generates for over 5s.

### 2. Low-beam light and take-me-home function control
`jac_s5_en_bfdedfcb` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When ignition switch is turned to ON position and BCM detects that low-beam light switch input is effective, low-beam light will light up.

2) When ignition switch is turned to ON position and BCM detects that automatic lamp switch input is effective and automatic lamp requests head lamp to turn on is effective, low-beam light lights up.

3) When ignition switch is turned to OFF and high-beam light switch ON to OFF triggers take-me-home function, one ignition cycle will actuate one time of take-me-home function. Next time when ignition condition is jumping to OFF position, take-me-home function can be reused and low-beam light will light up for 30s.

When the lamp lights up on above condition and relevant low-beam switch is detected, low-beam light will stop.

When the lamp lights up on above condition 2 and automatic lamp switch is turned off or request signal is ineffective, low-beam light will stop.

On above condition 3, when time is up and low-beam light is turned off, if ignition switch is jumping to other position than OFF position, low-beam light can also stop.

![](images/18b47b00119d3dcc115f5e5c5439034b69819efcddb2ba3e4f4aa953f900ccd7.webp)

### 3. High-beam light control
`jac_s5_en_a69f8d51` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When ignition switch is turned to ON position and BCM detects that high-beam light switch input is effective, high-beam light will light up.

2) When low-beam light is turned off and high-beam light goes out (ensured by hard wire), high-beam light can also be turned off separately.

3) Input of overtaking lamp is the same as high-beam light, but it has no the hard wire limit that low-beam light should be turned on first stated in above condition 2.

![](images/bc85c0513a64ebcce87c49cdc11ab9f2e58240385c364e0e9ab8bccfec0de65d.webp)

### 4. Position light control
`jac_s5_en_f1153f24` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Position light is divided into high and low configuration.

Low configuration: when ignition switch is placed on any position and position light switch input is effective or automatic lamp requests position light to start, position light, license light and back-light lamp output is turned on. When position light switch input is ineffective and automatic lamp request requests position light to stop, position light, license light and back-light lamp output is turned off.

When position light switch input is still effective and left front door is open, if unplugging key, small light will go out immediately; if left front door is closed and unplugging key, small light will not go out. Left front door is open and small light goes out, and then small light will light up if key is plug.

High configuration (with PEPS)

When ignition switch isn‘t placed on OFF position and position light switch input is effective or automatic lamp requests position light to start, position light, license light and back-light lamp output is turned on. When position light switch input is ineffective and automatic lamp request requests position light to stop or ignition switch is placed to OFF position, position light, license light and back-light lamp output is turned off.

![](images/01cc470c8cb000fa5d1f5801016953253bef03af1068825c8f0e3fd9985a114c.webp)

### 5. Daylight lamp control
`jac_s5_en_2ede39d4` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When engine is running and there is no low-beam light or position light output, turn on daylight lamp; otherwise, turn off it.

This function can only be seen in luxury vehicle model. The configuration value of daylight lamp must be set to co figurate this function of daylight lamp.

![](images/a9893ed7ab9a7bfe541d3790baca328eb61e723791a656b6fc62e01e10f8016f.webp)

### 6. Front fog control
`jac_s5_en_b0df1d7f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When ignition switch is placed to OFF, ACC, ON positions and position light lights up, press front fog lamp to light up front fog lamp. Front fog lamp switch is a normal switch.

2) Small light or front fog lamp switch is turned off, front fog lamp goes out.

![](images/14dc0ed85017c57444fc7cc9af278460acd3bf9aa51b413e8d21f57db026383a.webp)

### Condition of lighting up:
`jac_s5_en_0961c98f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When ignition switch is turned to ON position, front fog lamp or low-beam light lights up and rear fog lamp switch is effective.

### Condition of going out:
`jac_s5_en_25a63b02` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When front fog lamp or low-beam light or rear fog lamp switch is turned off, rear fog lamp goes out.
2) Ignition switch is turned to other positions than ON position.

![](images/407de35bdcba84ac632370537a3dcc022d23ad329064dfc6196cadd985c88dae.webp)

### 8. Automatic lamp
`jac_s5_en_6e375151` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:lighting | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

With automatic lamp, when it is dark outside and the lighting control switch stays on ―auto‖ position, small lights and low beams will turn on perceiving outside condition through light sensor.

AutolampDay _Cfg: 0-5V, initial value 1. 8V (the specific initial voltage threshold will be provided by the sensor), AutolampNight_Cfg: 0-5V, initial value 1.2V (the specific initial voltage threshold will be provided by the sensor)

1) At electrical initialization of BCM, it is night for automatic lamp by default;
2) When the automatic lamp sensor has an input voltage above AutolampDay_Cfg, it is identified as a day;
3) When the automatic lamp sensor has an input voltage below AutolampNight_Cfg, it is identified as a night;

### 9. Brake light control
`jac_s5_en_49fdb4b8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the ignition status is ACC or ON, the brake switch works or the braking request in the network is effective, then turn on brake light and high brake light and control with them separately.

In normal performance, the brake light and high brake light works through MCU for control or all brake switches can be turned on; less than 1 imphome mode, the brake light and high brake light can operate and control through brake switch.

### Output condition for interior lamp ON
`jac_s5_en_b6994a7b` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When door lights are at the control position and under any of the following circumstances, the Interior toplight turns on:

1) Protection released.
2) Ignition status at OFF and any door is open.
3) The key has been plugged out of the ignition switch.

With protection released, when the collision alarm satisfies any of the following conditions, the Interior toplight will turn off:

1) With protection released, the Interior toplight will turn off after working for 30s.
2) When the open door is closed, the Interior toplight will turn off after working for 30s.
3) When the key has been plugged out of the ignition switch, the Interior toplight will turn off after working for 30s.
4) The aforesaid conditions can be triggered afterward and burstore previous turn-on, while new timing is required.

When collision alarm is triggered, the Interior toplight turns on immediate being at the control position until any circumstance of items 1-3 above are triggered.

1) With protection on, the Interior toplight turns off immediately.
2) When doors are closed, the ignition status moves from 0FF/ACC to 0N/START and the Interior toplight turns off immediately.

### Under any of the following circumstances, the Interior toplight will turn off immediately:
`jac_s5_en_486af397` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When the closed door is open, Interior toplights will stay on; when the open time is >15min, the Interior toplight goes off immediately.
2) The duration for Interior toplight to turn off is 5.5± 0.5s.

![](images/a43deee6a632a5e6604b07a9fcd892ed1521c7e33357a2bb70a02f1d0e7a9cd0.webp)

### 11. Keyhole light
`jac_s5_en_0e981adf` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

With key not inserted in the ignition switch, the keyhole light will turns on when the door on the driver side is open; when the key in ignition switch is turned to position ―ON‖ or the door is closed, the keyhole light will be on for 10s before going off.

![](images/b3c2fe52cac3a8dff686b12a0b75930c9bde1a1a61cb5fcaff3ca200b98519c0.webp)

### 12. Front wiper
`jac_s5_en_3a7b8e7b` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) When the key is placed in the ignition switch at position ―ON‖, put the control switch for front wiper at low speed, high speed and burst position to engage relevant switch, then the front wiper will operate accordingly.

2) When the wiper is put at burst, the duration of burst cant be put in five settings, as shown below:

![](images/6bd1d75be99764d23f85005fc67676f9559f0bbe62f2044c962761790002d220.webp)

With speed >100km/h, to apply burst mode, deduct 4s from the setting duration.

3) High and low speed control for wiper: when the switch is engaged at low speed, the low speed relay picks up and the motor runs at high speed.
4) When the inching switch is triggered, the wiper wipes at low speed.
5) When front water spray <400ms, wiper will not actuate, while >400ms, the wiper will keep wiping and takes one more wipe after stop of water spray.
6) Reset control of front wiper: when the switch is turned off, with motor not running to the initial position, BCM receives no reset signal and the high speed relay is off while the low speed relay is still on, then the motor keeps running; when the motor running to the initial position, the reset switch is turned off, ETACS receives reset signal and low speed relay is off, then the motor stops running.
7) During the interval, sprinkler interlocking works first.

Blocking protection for front wiper

During operation, when the homing switch of front wiper is detected to fail to move within 18s, then the motor must have been blocked, stop output in this case. At this time, only when the ignition switch takes a 0N-0FF-0N cycle or the wiper switch moves off from OFF, the wiper will start again.

![](images/4c0815ba638327ec9b125b21945101479f08fa28571135fc3418712d2457a54e.webp)

### 13. Rear wiper
`jac_s5_en_9bb44eb2` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) It‘s not allowed to adjust the interval of rear wiper, which is 3s, it will wipes slowly at slow speed position.

2) The rear sprinkling switch will not activate when <600ms; it will move after continuous activation for 600ms which is the shortest cycle for wiper; when the sprinkling duration is 3s longer than the cycle, the wiper will wipe for another cycle.

3) During the interval, sprinkler interlocking goes first.

![](images/39bd978fe5d45f0179775d5bfb8faa87e792d94ac77da4e3deab136dc901def7.webp)

### 14. Locking system
`jac_s5_en_583eb098` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The locking system controls locks through relay inside BCM and achieves the following functions:

1) Central locking and unlocking;
2) Remote locking and unlocking;
3) Locking and unlocking with key;
4) When the speed exceeds the preset value (40km/h by default, setting in EEPR0M), doors will
be locked automatically;
5) Auto relocking;
6) Back door unlocking;
7) Forced unlocking by collision.

14.1. Functional description of locks

Protected state: BCM state after successful RKE remote locking or locking with key on the driver side, with all doors closed.

Unprotected state: the state after remote unlocking or unlocking with key on the driver side or remote unlocking of back door.

### 14.2. RKE transmitter features
`jac_s5_en_f02a3542` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the key insert signal works, there‘s no response to RKE remote locking and unlocking command.

Button Operation Functional description Unlock Press one time Unlock four doors Lock Press one time Lock four doors Unlock back door Press and hold Release the protection but not unlock

### 14.3. Door lock
`jac_s5_en_b03985c6` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Doors are controlled by the relay inside SJB, i.e. having received effective unlocking signal, SJB will operate to control the motor for door lock, which yields out put time 250ms for performing unlocking. The door lock is provided with internal lock motor, which has two mechanic states:

Unlocked state: accessible from both inside and outside;

Locked state: only accessible from inside;

RKE or driver side door locking with key

Under unprotected condition, when BCM receives effective RKE locking signal or key locking signal for driver side door, if all four doors and back door are closed, then it will lock the four doors and Turning lights on both sides flash once for 1s, including 0.5s on and 0.5s off. In case BCM receives RKE unlocking command at this moment, the light flashing turns into unlocking flashing process; when the door on the driver side is closed while any of the other doors is not closed, it will lock the motor while the Turning light will not flash. When all doors have been closed, another movement will be taken for confirmation, i.e. the Turning lights on both sides will flash once for 1, including 0.5s on and 0.5s off.

Under protected condition, when BCM receives effective RKE locking signal, it will complete unlocking and action for confirmation altogether, i.e. lock the motor and the two side Turning lights flash once at a cycle of 1s, including 0.5s on and 0.5s off.

![](images/45bf281784696872f3dbd5d660e46e53e1929d782185f5f45043c1e9c07842b5.webp)

### RKE or driver side door unlocking with key
`jac_s5_en_9b67d413` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Under protected condition, when BCM receives effective RKE unlocking signal or key locking signal for driver side door, doors are unlocked, with protection released, and further actions are taken for confirmation, i.e. Turning lights on both sides flash twice before going off.

Under unprotected condition, when BCM receives effective RKE unlocking signal, BCM operates to unlock and turning lights takes no further action for confirmation.

![](images/dba8392b96a6c08cd10daf1d05f1be4e13d4bd4bbf1336af6cff7f87a627e1ef.webp)

### Auto locking function
`jac_s5_en_f8864cff` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the speed reaches the preset value (40km/h by default, set in EEPROM), if the lock motor on the driver side is under unlocked state while the driver side door is closed, BCM will lock the door automatically.

![](images/018c32c141f5ff19ea1a42b1564f52e124d58f934acbca4014eb3e5288e481be.webp)

### Auto relocking
`jac_s5_en_19a325e7` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

In case the door fails to open within 30s after protection released, and then repeat RKE locking procedures (please refer to RKE locking).

![](images/212a4d1f9a74fbe5cda86a6c1ae98b2de5eb7fd2d8bc3b62ff38a7beb257f661.webp)

### RKE back door locking
`jac_s5_en_533e0805` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When RKE remote unlocking signal for back door is received under protected condition, then the protection will be released and unlocking signal will be accepted to perform back door unlocking and motor locking in 500ms.

When back door unlocking signal is received under unprotected condition, back door lock motor will activate to unlock in 500ms.

![](images/d3086dd4c2a911553bae97256e5ae2a6d9a4659dc3ed91a2412ae57bf55f2463.webp)

### Central control switch locking /unlocking
`jac_s5_en_832623cb` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Central control switch:

When BCM receives central locking signal under unprotected condition, it will lock the four doors if all doors and back door are closed. If the driver side door is not closed, the lock motor will not activate; if other doors are not closed, lock motor will activate. If the lock motor is already locked, then when BCM receives central locking signal, the lock motor will active again for unlocking.

![](images/42e9caf7c2dab96258f95561976529f5a63d93bfda26197037d52ddfa777056a.webp)

Central control switches unlocking:

Under unprotected state, when BCM receives central unlocking signal, it will activate four-door motors to unlock. Incase four doors are at unlocked state, when BCM receives central unlocking signal, the lock motor will active again for unlocking..

![](images/cfe8b3c116a11ddfb7dd87556d8c82417a84ae451245a87b52f7260e0a7c72bd.webp)

### Forced unlocking by collision
`jac_s5_en_e8f07c3f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the ignition switch is put at ―ON‖, if BCM receives effective collision alarm, it will unlock protected doors by force and shield auto locking, central locking and RKE remote locking features.

When BCM detects the ignition switch shifts from OFF to ON, auto locking, central locking and RKE locking will be restored.

![](images/c06e1c31348974ce16c9212db1ecb463a08cf5186eef8a9e0984823ad80c1d28.webp)

### Misoperation preventing lock
`jac_s5_en_2c041927` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the key has been put into the ignition lock, the door on the driver side is open, if locking command is detected as the driver side lock state signal, then implement locking right before unlocking for 3 times at an interval of 0.5s.

![](images/2ceecbdb5dcd127a309a4919159f5e3549844c9cda692da2c64cb5a2f749e92f.webp)

### Locking mechanism protection
`jac_s5_en_e5c6a707` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The lock counter will add 1 to every time it receives a locking/ unlocking signal; when it reaches the value N=8 within 10s, it will stop locking for a certain time, i.e. Config. LockThermalDelayCfg (15s by default) before recovery, and stay under unlocked state when thermal protection is triggered.

### 15. Anti-theft alarm system
`jac_s5_en_fd0e771f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The alarm block gives alarm features for the vehicle under different abnormal conditions; once it is triggered, it will activate sound-light alarm to remind the user and control starting function.

### Details about alarm function:
`jac_s5_en_aa0f319f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) With all doors closed, lock the door with remote control or key on the driver side, the hazard warning light flashes for T2 (1.0±0.2sec) and alarm condition is triggered; when no door is closed after the driver side door is closed, use remote control to lock the door and when doors are locked, alarm condition is cleared while it is triggered when all doors are closed.

2) Under alarm condition, with 5 doors open or key insertion signal detected, alarm will sound out and restriction function will be triggered. It works in the same way to trigger multiple alarms at the same time.
3) Use remote control or key for door on the driver side to unlock the door and clear alarm condition, when doors are open, hazard warning light flashes for Tl (0.5±0.1seC) and another T1 after an interval T2 (0. 5±0. lsec).
4) Clear the alarm condition with remote unlocking signal for back door, then the hazard warning light flashes for Tl(0.5±0.lsec) and another T1 after an interval T2 (0.5 + 0. lsec).
5) Under alarm condition, when the door is open, the alarm whistle sounds for Tl (27±2sec) and another T1 after an interval T2(10±lseC) while no repeated alarm is applied; the hazard warning light flashes for T3 (0.5±0. lsec) at a duty ratio 50% as the same for alarm whistle.
6) Under alarm condition, when the door is closed, press the ―lock‖ button on the remote control to exit alarm condition; when all doors are locked, the Turning light flashes once and anti-theft relay is off, then the alarm condition is triggered again.
7) Under alarm condition, with door open, press ―lock‖ button on the remote control to excit alarm condition; all doors are locked, anti-theft relay is stopped and the horn stops alarming, then after all doors are closed, the Turning light flashes once and return to alarm condition.
8) Under alarm condition, use remote unlocking or mechanical unlocking with key to clear the alarm and the unlocking relay outputs unlocking signal, then doors are unlocked; when the alarm condition is cleared, the hazard warning light flashes twice and the alarm whistle stops whistling.
9) When power is applied again after power dump, it shall restore the condition before power down.
10) Under protected condition, clear the protection through remote unlocking signal for back door; when tailgate unlocking signal from CAN is received, unlock the trunk while protection will be restored after the trunk is detected to have been closed. In case the protection is not restored within 30s after protection release, set and restore the protection immediately after back door is closed again and all the other doors are closed; after the restoration is done, the warning light will flash for T2 (1. 0±0. 2sec) for confirmation. When the back door is not closed, wait until it was closed before resetting and restoration.
11) Under protected condition, release the protection through remote locking or locking with key on the driver side and the protection will restored in 30s in case no door is open;

### Details about alarm function:
`jac_s5_en_2265e473` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

12) Under warning condition, the anti-theft horn is triggered for 100ms.

![](images/7fe9c4322aa4f44c8165db2c19631b7f064ad75bad7fa0d1b226c79f8c3af242.webp)

T1: 200 ms T2: 1.0±0.2s

![](images/c27f234053ab9c91b38f9e7b45635c563d706227fa4398de1373e465e62586b2.webp)

T1: 27±2sec T2: 10±1sec T3: 0.5±0. 1sec

### Detailed description of defrosting function
`jac_s5_en_aa72d07d` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

With generator terminal L working and effective rear defrosting signal from the air condition controller received, the rear demister starts heating for 20 minutes before stopping automatically. During the process, press rear demister button to stop heating.

With generator terminal L working, press front demister button, then the front demister starts heating for 20 minutes before stopping automatically. Press front demister button to stop heating with process.

### 17. Operation of electric window
`jac_s5_en_6dea7efc` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Applicable for electric window power supply control

### 18. Counting of ignition frequency
`jac_s5_en_7ad33db4` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the ignition switch is detected to have finished a cycle from OFF/ACC to ON, the counter will add and restart counting from 0 after running over.

### 20. Anti-clamp motor control
`jac_s5_en_0ae16bf8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Through LIN bus, follow LIN2.0 protocol, take BCM as the host node, and use the first LIN.

This feature is not available for the foundation, only for standard and luxurious models, where smart configuration is required for anti-clamp motor.

### 21. Parking Sensor Alarm Control
`jac_s5_en_c91a9ecd` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Through LIN bus, take BCM as the host node, LIN connection block diagram shown below:

![](images/283bd38897d3d81397158673a9d5c98e1bc437e06968d9193855f7b2aa270abf.webp)

The buzzer frequency of 4 probes is collected first and it is considered invalid when the distance between right and left probes ≥60cm, so the buzzing signal will not be taken, or otherwise it is considered effective and buzzing will start for the probe which shows the highest buzzer frequency; When the buzzer frequency for the last probe is received, the first distance will be discarded and comparison will be made between the 2nd to the 4th probe, as a result, buzzing will sound for the one considered having the highest buzzer frequency and so on.

### Operation of BCM remote key
`jac_s5_en_719db5d8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

RKE carry out programming by diagnosis method, detail match programming key method as follow:

![](images/1452f206dafef9f134c1efb913d1d9d6b9c2ac277cb14ff34933db492070c1db.webp)

### Program the key
`jac_s5_en_5b0208a0` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) You can learn operation procedures for two keys in Routine;
2) When Routine >2min., BCM exits the Routine and learned key will be memorized by
EEPR0M;
3) The first finished key will be recorded as the first one;
4) ―Unlock‖ and ―lock‖ keys have to be pressed together during learning;
5) Exit conditions: the 2 minutes runs out; diagnosis end command provided.

To program a key:

Enter key programming procedure Right side turning light is always ―ON‖ press and hold ―lock‖ and ―unlock‖ buttons for 2s, at this time, left turning light on the instrument panel turn on, then click ―OK‖ on the diagnostic instrument to confirm programming while left and right turning indicator lights are turn off, then the programming is done.

![](images/ad5c7b01d20eb3d21398aaae6225cb947362c2ade4e33c6401d9c3784bf3cc53.webp)

To program two keys:

Enter key programming procedure Right side turning light is always ―ON‖ press and hold ―lock‖ and ―unlock‖ buttons for 2s for the first key, then turning light on the instrument turns on after the programming is done for the first key, do not press ―OK‖ button on the diagnostic instrument to confirm and continue to program the second key by pressing and holding ―lock‖ and ―unlock‖ buttons for 2s, then left and right turning indicator lights on the instrument turns off, click ―OK‖ on the diagnostic instrument to confirm programming and the programming for two keys are done.

Op

![](images/afa1b4848b8ec57535943976997512892e90663d9c885f3879b5894c0eb4402c.webp)

### Review
`jac_s5_en_0f0dbd31` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. It is able to rise or decline corresponding window glass in the electric window system by operates the electric window switch on every door trim plate.
2. The master switch on the driver side door trim plate can use to rise or decline window glass on every passsenger side and lock every switch there accordingly.
3. Only when the ignition switch is at ―ON‖ position, the electric window system will take battery voltage from circuit breaker in the junction box.
4. The electric window system consists of electric window switches on door trims, BCM control module and electric window motors in every door.
5. The window motor and glass lifter constitute an assembly. When it is necessary to replace window motor, the window lifter has to be replaced too.

### Elements Description
`jac_s5_en_410caf82` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/129e7f0efeeeb678c312b74244b510ace112786730bb9b2fdedd23cf2768e140.webp)
Schemcaitc diagram of electric window switch

1. Window un locking switch 2. Window locking switch 3. Left front door window UN and DOWN switch 4. Right front door window UN and DOWN switch 5. Left back door window UN and DOWN switch 6 .Right back door window UN and DOWN switch

### 1. Electric window switch
`jac_s5_en_983390fe` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) The electric window is controlled through electric window switch on front door trim plates. The driver side electric window switch enables the driver to control all electric windows. All windows on the driver side receive power supply from battery and ground wire power through window switch circuit. When the lock on the driver side electric window switch is ―lock‖, the power supply from battery is cut off for electric window switch of all windows, except the driver side electric window switch.
2) The Auto Down left front door lifter has one-button down function, the window glass can be lowered by pressing the switch in two ways: inching down and one-button down; to lift up the switch, the glass will raise only in inching up way.

3) The Auto left front door lifter has both one-button down and anti-clamp feature, i.e. descending by pressing the switch in two ways: inching down and one-button down to lift up the switch, the glass will raise in inching up and one-button up ways.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_05b22c2f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

For Auto lifter, whenever the battery power supply is cut off, got through the learning process again as follows: connect ignition switch, operate glass lifter UP switch repeatedly to raise the glass to the top and descend it then before the lifter acquires one-button up and down and anti-clamp features.

### 2. Electric window motor
`jac_s5_en_94e5e9c0` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The reversible permanent magnet motor drives window glass lifter to move through ingral gearbox. The motor moves to the same direction through connection of its terminals to the negative and positive battery while the reverse current passing through two same connections drives the motor to move in reverse direction. Furthermore, every electric window motor is equipped with an integral auto circuit breaker to prevent motor being overloaded.

3. When the ignition switch is turned off, electric window is still operatable, but not
whenever any of the following circumstances arise:
1) The ignition switch turns off or over 30s.
2) Any door is open.
3) The door is locked from outside.

Lock electric window

The electric window lock switch can disable electric window button on right front door, left back door and right back door.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_9a56b02f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Do not apply lifter anti-clamp feature without stop in case of finger pinch after the lifter goes into protected mode.
Do not turn on the master switch and secondary switch along different directions, or the window glass will just stops, capable of neither being closed nor opened.
Take care to close window glass, in case of pinch by the glass.
When a child sits in rear seat, please apply electric window lock switch.

### Overview:
`jac_s5_en_61331622` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The electric window anti-clamp system controls electric window by sensing its extrusion force.

Extrusion force sensing: monitoring window movement with Hall element, determining the extrusion force acting on the glass through monitoring the load of electric window glass lifter motor; when the extrusion force is monitored approaching the critical value 100 N specified by regulations, the anti-clamp module for electric window will generate and send command to stop motor or make it move reversely.

System composition: Hall element embedded motor & anti-clamp module for electric window (integrated on the motor).

![](images/1e6b67eb34d7f805abd51fa519aad04c12fd81d663cfd06110dfee02e330103b.webp)
Schematic diagram

### SR electric sunroof Composition of electric sunroof
`jac_s5_en_6415c3d7` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/bca801aeb64e4abe92bb08af42f2e29bab0213903d00b04b614ccd8087ff4e51.webp)
