### Operation of electric sunroof
`jac_s5_en_4c2df480` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When ignition switch only located in the ―ON‖ position, it can open or close front ceiling light or sunroof by sunroof switch

![](images/f1cd0139c5896fcb6cdf91a59eb230642b06763be0730350b9adaa214632669b.webp)

Electric operation items and action mode

![](images/6ff6a415b16481ba055730ad0de6952811276f34362d23b05f605ffd0fb0fc5e.webp)

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_75979f47` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the position information is not set up, the following circumstances will occur and it is necessary to reset position.

1. Sunroof glass does not stop movement when at proper position.
2. No auto movement.
3. Anti-clamp and roll-back protection features fail.

< Initialization handling method >

① Turn off SW/Down, SW=ON
② Set the position when the motor is detected to be blocked along ―OFF‖ direction, which is
identified as fully closed mechanical locking position.
③ Auto UP at fully closed position.
④ Stop at fully closed position and initialization is done.

【Tip】 During initialization (①〜④), operate SW=ON.

Operate SW = OFF in the process, it will restart operation from the step ①.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_0605d40b` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Do not close the sunroof by your hands, arm or any other part of your body is in the middle. When the sunroof is open, do not extend your head or arm out, or your hand or arm may collide on something during travelling and causes death or serious injury. Do not allow the passenger to stand up or put anything out of the sunroof when the car is travelling. In cold season, when the sunroof is covered with snow or ice, do not open it. To clear dust, take care that some dust may stay and accumulate in the guide rail. After cleaning or raining, wipe out water on the top before operating sunroof in case of water penetration because it may lead to rusting or damage to inner roof lining.

### Sunroof Sunvisor
`jac_s5_en_9e42c573` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Sunroof sunvisor can be opened or closed with hand.

When the sunroof glass is open, sunvisor will open automatically, but then closed manually.

![](images/77c8c7439e2dddba8cf90f956fb4992db07beef6120c006ce2fd122c533d5148.webp)

### Assemble and disassemble sunroof assembly
`jac_s5_en_73ae672a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/e653c398a895dbf9fc056a789f83b11e15bed0e4f16aa0a173bc9e6abd42c450.webp)

Disassemble the following parts:

Trim plate on post A, B and C, top handrail, sunvisor, ceiling.
Slide-rail check strips for both left and right sunroofs.

Plug out sunroof water pipe. Remove sunroof fixing bolts and bolt at the torque:

4 N·m ± 0.5N·m

④Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_c8b0c88a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Assembly and disassembly requires at least two people in cooperation.
Put on gloves to protect your hands and do not scratch other parts during disassembly.
Drain pipe shall run unhindered, bent or twisted.
Before connecting the drain pipe to sunroof, match and assemble pipe clamp and make drain pipe is overlapped with the sunroof for ≥15mm being connected.

### Assemble and disassemble glass
`jac_s5_en_eda28933` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Remove left right slide-rail check strip. Remove glass fixing screws. Lift up the glass to remove it. Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly

`jac_s5_en_aa386940` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

### Adjust sunroof glass
`jac_s5_en_0745e08a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

After installation of sunroof assembly, adjust the flushness between glass sealing strip and roof metal plate as shown in the figure.

Requirement: flushness A: O-l mm, the glass sealing strip plane shall be flat and level to or higher than roof metal plate (height A).

Unscrew bolts on both side of the glass while adjust the position at the height and in the front and rear of glass till proper tension before tightening bolts

Tightening torque: 4N\*m〜7N\*m.

### Description of Combination Switch Turning light switch
`jac_s5_en_1a398c8d` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Turning light will not work unless the ignition switch is ―ON‖.

To turn left, please pull down the control lever of turning light switch while pull it up to turn right. At this moment, the direction indicator light on the combination instrument flashes together.

When the vehicle drives out of curves and the steering wheel returns to standard forward position, turn signal light switch and indicator light will return automatically and turn off.

![](images/d2dda0d336b85a41dc3f5eb70c0ab11f07a50e9c483e67a8bf5c5199e2241a7e.webp)

### Headlamp switch
`jac_s5_en_48517299` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Turn the cylindrical knob at the end of the control lever of turning light to control lights. Turn the knob to small light, then small lights, license plate light and instrument back-light light as well as console back-light light turns on. Turn the knob to headlamp position, headlamps turn on.

When headlamp switch stays at any of the aforesaid positions, take out the key and small lights will stay on.

Please check if the headlamp switch is at ―OFF‖position before stepping out of your car in case of batter electric loss or starting fault.

![](images/e67a5520a847e8fdd23f53255f835f07b111a9d5589c55b19e993889bc1de893.webp)

### High beam light switch
`jac_s5_en_62bdf19c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Turn the cylindrical knob at the end of the control lever of turning light to high beam light position and push the control lever forward till a click is heard, then the high beam headlamp turns on. A t the same time, the high beam indicator light on the instrument turns on. To switch back to low beam, pull back the control lever.

### Headlamp flash
`jac_s5_en_35382f1b` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

To flash high beam, pull back the control lever of turning light switch tenderly and release it, then the high beam light turns on and goes off later.

Even when the control lever of turning light switch is at ―OFF‖ position, headlamps can flash too. Pull back and hold the control lever of turning light switch, then high beam will stay on as long as you hold the lever.

### Front & rear fog lamp switch
`jac_s5_en_4e9503d5` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Front & Rear fog lamp switch is on the turning light control lever. When the circular rotary switch at the end stays at high beam light position, you can turn the knob in the middle of control lever to ―ON‖ position to turn on front and rear fog lamp, then the front & rear fog lamp indicator on the dashboard turns on as a reminder; turn the knob to ―OFF‖ position, the front fog lamp turns off.

![](images/5b6f0a836d1997c857e8686dda9373d3a43e5c175db6cfea35eee1c775f657fc.webp)

### Windshield wiper and washer
`jac_s5_en_43c632f4` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

This control lever is used to control windshield wiper and washer. When the ignition switch stays at ―ON‖ position, the windshield wiper and washer is operatable. This control lever is designed with five gears:

MIST: inching mode OFF: Turnoff INT: Intermittent L0: Wiping at low speed HI: Wiping at high speed

![](images/a14dedd3102b5992101f6706d5f17b65ed713015049518497cbcfa100c4d1966.webp)

To operate the wiper in mist, push up the control lever from ―OFF‖ position, then the wiper will wipe at high speed until you release the control lever. In this case, it can wipe clean the windshield fast.

At Intermittent position, the wiper wipes left and rights every 5s. At low and high speed positions, the wipe will wipe continuously at different speed.

To use windshield washer, pull wiper control lever inward, then the washer will keep spraying water and the wiper will swing at low speed until you release the control lever. After you release the control lever, the wiper will give another wiping before stop.

In winter or freezing weather, check if the wiper blade has been frozen on the windshield before use of wiper

![](images/b84c6aeb10828a042730a9721308250083dae3b4c79b3b54bf36ed38db7703b6.webp)

### Caution
`jac_s5_en_ab116591` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

〇The washer shall work for no more than 15s every time and you must not operate the windshield washer when no scrubbing solution is stored in the tank.

〇In the area where it is apt to freeze and ice in winter, use antifreezing windshield scrubbing solution.

〇To operate the wiper at lower temperature, check if the wiper blade has been frozen on the windshield, or it will cause the motor to be burnt down.

〇If the wiper stops working due to ice or other obstructions on the windshield during operation, it may causes the wiper motor to be burnt down. In this case, stop the vehicle immediately and turn of the ignition switch, then clean windshield to allow wiper to work smoothly.

〇When it is dry on the surface of windshield, do not use the wiper, or it will scratch the glass and causes early wear and tear of blade.

### Replacement of combination switch:
`jac_s5_en_9fbbe55a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Remove the negative pole of battery and wait for min. 3 minutes before removing electric connectors, or it will cause damage to the vehicle.
To disassemble or assemble components, cover the component to remove with clothing in case of damage.
To remove metal clamp from the trimming, wrap the edge of slotted screwdriver with clothing. Take care not to damage body components.
To install body trimmings, confirm if the clamp has been well established in the board hole on the body before pressing them in carefully.
For disassembly of large components which can not be accomplished by a single person, find two people to operate together in case of falling.
To remove some trimmings, do not apply too much force because it may causes parts deformation.

### Disassembly procedure
`jac_s5_en_7cd750fb` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Turn off the ignition switch, disconnect negative pole of battery and wait for 1 minute at least;

2)

Remove the horn hood;

![](images/dd75facbfe59d58cf7921571214d316fc7d814e3255f3319ac34f669d88d97c2.webp)

Remove the steering wheel;

Disassemble air bag clock spring;

5) Screw off the two fixing screws on combination switch;

![](images/8abfdba5473a7dbef3de3f9bcfe0ca14ff247edeae5b3b91fba4365c49526142.webp)

6) Disconnect combination switch wiring harness connector;

Remove the combination switch.

2. Installation procedure Assemble it in the reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

![](images/03fa60314c621e0fa27bb7d890b49041a7f46b14bb23c76f02bc2475632c89d6.webp)

### Operate the lighting handle for combination switch and inspect all lights and switches for proper op
`jac_s5_en_40957392` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:lighting | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. When the rotating lighting handle is put on ―OFF‖, all external lamps and internal night lights for electrical equipments shall be off.
2. When the rotating lighting handle is put on small light position, position lights, front and rear small lights, license plate light, instrument lightings and night light for internal electrical equipment shall be on and when in traveling, side marker lamp will be on too.
3. When the rotating lighting handle is put on small light position, press the front (rear) fog lamp button, all front (rear) fog lamps will be on.
4. When the rotating lighting handle is put on headlight position, the low beam of head lamp is on and all small lights stay on. At this moment, turn the handle down for 6°, high beam of head lamp is on; then turn the handle to the original position and turn it up for 6°, the high beam is on instantaneously and the switch returns automatically when the handle is released.
5. Turn the lighting handle forward (rearward) for 15°, all right (left) turning lights start flashing and right (left) turning indicator lights flashes too, then turn the handle to the previous position, the lamps stop flashing; turn the handle forward (rear) for 9°, right (left) turning lights flash instantaneously and returns when the handle is released.
6. Press the alarm button, all turning lights will flash together while direction indicator lights flash at the same time.

### Operation of combination switches for wiping/washing, inspecting wiper and washer working condition:
`jac_s5_en_44d5ae73` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. When the handle is at ―OFF‖, the wiper and wiper and washer will not actuate.
2. When the handle is at ―MIST‖, wiper makes inching movement; when the action force disappears, the handle returns and wiper stops working.
3. When the handle is at ―INT‖, the wiper blade moves intermittently at certain interval.
4. When the handle is at ―L0‖, the wiper blade moves continuously at low speed.
5. When the handle is at ―HI‖, the wiper blade moves continuously at high speed.
6. Lift up the handle, front washer nozzle sprays water on the front windshield while the front wiper is working; when the action force is withdrawn, the handle returns automatically and both sprinkler and wiper stops working.
7. Turn the knob at the end of handle to position "INT‖ and rear wiper starts to move at low speed.
8. Turn the knob at the end of handle to position "ON‖, rear wiper starts to move at high speed.
9. Push down the handle, rear washer nozzle sprays water on the rear windshield while the rear wiper is working; when the action force is withdrawn, the handle returns automatically and both sprinkler and wiper stops working.

### Attention
`jac_s5_en_83739366` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Follow all instructions on operation of air bag system strictly, or it may cause damage to vehicle or personal injury.
Remove the negative pole of battery and wait for at least 3 minutes before removing electric connectors, or it will cause damage to the vehicle.
To disassemble or assemble components, cover the component to remove with clothing in case of damage.
To remove metal clamp from trimming, cover the edge of slotted screwdriver with clothing. Take care not to damage body elements.
To install body trimmings, make sure the clamp is placed securely within the hole on body panel and then carefully press them in first.
In case it is impossible for you to work alone on certain component removement, find a partner to work together in case of falling.
Do not apply too much force in disassembling certain trimmings because it may lead to deformation or damage of parts.
To work on bulbs, pull up the hand brake, turn ignition switch to "LOCK" position and turn off lighting.
Be sure to use a new bulb of the same rated wattage to replace the burnt-out one, or it will cause damage to fuse or electrical system.
When driving in strom or after vehicle cleaning, frosting may appear on headlamps and tail light hood due to temperature difference inside and outside the lam. It is similar to condensed fog on the interior window during raining, but not necessarily indicates a fault. The frosting will vanish shortly in operation. To get rid of it immediately, just turn on the lamp for 0. 5〜1H. When the engine stops running, do not use inner lamps for long, or it will lead to battery discharge.
Do not put goods except for glasses in the spectacle case for reading light, or it will be cast out in case of emergency brake or accidents and may hurt passengers inside. Do not open the spectacle case during driving as it will block the view from rearview mirror.

### Replacement of headlamp:

1. Disassembly procedure
1) Disconnect the negative pole of battery;

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_be4f89d6` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

After installation, check if the light meets requirements. If no, please adjust refer to ―Headlamp light adjustment‖

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_b34f4448` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Do not take the headlamp out of reflector for long, because dust, moisture, mist or smoke may penetrate into the lamp and affect its performance. Assemble a new bulb right after removal of headlamp bulb from the reflector. Headlamps are semi-closed light beam light s and replaceable halide lamp shall be used. You can replace the bulb in engine compartment, but it‘s better to replace it after removal. To operate on the bulb, just hold its plastic base, never touch the glass shell. Do not shake or turn the bulb in removal. It is necessary to adjust the illuminating point of headlamp after reassembly of front lamps.
1. Disassembly Procedure:
1) Disconnect the negative pole of battery, Remove the headlamp assembly;

2) Directly plug out the rear rubber cover of low beam light;

![](images/98f3b98dc9c1a9d8146ef1f252fc90edf37d890bc85b02c7d34935c4f99a8e0e.webp)

3) Unscrew fastener (snap spring) & disconnect wires;

4 Remove low beam light bulb carefully.

![](images/7ba1eef34a70e1b1baf8f534ddf2662de1c6696a2cdbd7231aeb714b64491ba8.webp)

2. Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

Replace bulb of headlamp turning light:

Disassembly procedure: Disconnect the negative pole of battery, Remove the headlamp assembly; 2) Screw off turning light holder counterclockwise;

3) Press down the bulb slightly and screw out turning light bulb counterclockwise.

![](images/c7fb0a549a83eeafc315a6facb47f13be35a68f93d62215c057dfb08226fd197.webp)

2. Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

Replace headlamp high beam light & small light bulbs:

1. Disassembly procedure: 1) Disconnect the negative pole of battery, remove the headlamp assembly;

2) Unplug the rubber cover of lamp holder ;

3) Unscrew the fastener& detach connectors;

![](images/67ba53c2cf206d77095d3ff1f9f6741b09eb14ba41e02993588eb739f251930f.webp)

4) Remove high beam & small light bulbs.

2. Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

### Headlamp light adjustment:
`jac_s5_en_0a67c649` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Check and make sure all tires are under proper pressure.
2) Place the vehicle and tester on a flat level surface.
3) Check and make sure the vehicle has no load other than the driver (or equivalent objects are put
in the driver position) (refill fluid to proper level, e.g. coolant & engine oil).
4) Turn on headlamp low beam light.
5) Use adjusting screw to adjust beam focusing.

Low & high beams are adjustable Up/Down in dimmer fastener while left/right through two fasteners. After repair of the front of vehicle and / or replacement of headlamp assembly, check beam focusing and use light detector to make proper adjustment.

6) Focus the beam to proper extent based on focusing schedule.

![](images/959f01508601407e4356683a248e49d4d3c2d3b6ebc0aff26bb2ad1f62f16e4f.webp)

W1: headlamp bulb center distance (low beam) H1: Height of headlamp bulb above the ground (low beam) W2: headlamp bulb center distance (high beam H2: Height of headlamp bulb above the ground (High beam) W3: fog lamp bulb center distance H3: Height of fog lamp bulb center above the ground L: Clearance between headlamp bulb and wall

1. Turn on low beam when no driver sits in the car, the cut-off line will shows projection within the allowable range (shaded area)

Unit： mm

![](images/f8db481abbc20fae3c94f4df39e1e58dcd46ea4a72de6fef11df3007a2a58342.webp)

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_5afdb0ad` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:lighting | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Front fog lamp beam has to be adjusted to provide proper lighting on the road. To install a new fog lamp assembly maintain or repair the front side of vehicle may destabilize the mounting support for front fog lamp; as a result, the front fog lamp must be checked for beam adjustment.

![](images/32be5146bb6c5f72172a1722037e61b7df9092f7a698d69e403bd488235f12e6.webp)

The beam from front fog lamp assembly can not be adjusted horizontally for this model.

### Beam adjustment position schematic diagram of front fog lamp
`jac_s5_en_ebd90c77` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) To make sure precise adjustment of normal beam of front fog lamp, please follow the following procedures to get the car ready:
① In case of any other maintenance, please firstly make sure all parts are completed in the correct
position
② Make sure fuel is at the highest allowable level or lower slightly.
③ Place the car on a level ground, 1.52m away from the target screen.
④ Stop all other operations on the vehicle.
⑤ Shake suspension to put it in place.
⑥ Cover the headlamp in beam focusing for fog lamp.
2) Turn on front fog lamp.
3) Insert Phillips screwdriver below front bumper to access vertical adjusting screws of fog lamp.
4) Adjust fog lamp up and down to make the top edge of high light area on the screen is 102mm lower than horizontal center circuit (2), varying at the range from 0 mm below the horizontal center circuit (3) to the ground circuit (5) on target screen.
5) Turn off front fog lamp.

`jac_s5_en_c3880f0d` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

During assembly, do not damage the tail light.
After assembly, check if the tail light was installed in the correct position, if it is in proper fit clearance with the body, if not well installed or improper, reassemble it. Cover up the service cover plate after
connection of connectors.

### Reading light
`jac_s5_en_68fcd249` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Reading light emits beam for night reading or provide private lighting for the second driver. Front top light is provided with three levels: left, middle and right (Left ON in the figure; middle DOOR and right OFF respectively). At ―ON‖ position, apply electric to the front top light, it will stay on until manual turn-off. Operate at DOOR position, the top light will be on whenever a door is open (except the back door); when the door is closed, top light will go off. Operate at OFF position, top light will turn off completely (even under the condition that a door is open).

### 1. Description
`jac_s5_en_992f13f6` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Rearview mirror includes inside and outside rear-view mirrors, fixed on top of front windshield inside and out of body on both Left and Right, used to observe conditions within the compartment and in rear of the vehicle.

### 2. Element location diagram
`jac_s5_en_2b2e2532` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/efeb7255d6309b446f6d021aed7946b5c5230c5436a9ac399ea9e66e75754241.webp)

1. Inside rear-view mirror assembly

![](images/222740e4355acc18ecfec09a0301cc3f5d17426925f6e6d3d801d84cdddc5218.webp)

2.Outside rear-view mirror switch

3. Outer rear-view mirror assembly

![](images/84017a8dd7f606e56597a892d6936fbe73d9ea4b0cca9347aefeaaca3afc76bd.webp)

1. Left outside rear-view mirror assembly
2. Hex bolt, spring washer and flat washer assembly
3. Pre-suspended steel clamp

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_ec555bb4` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

It is only allowed to adjust rearview mirror when the ignition switch stays at ―ON‖ (electric -on) position.

Adjustment of outside rear-view mirror:

![](images/2fe3be196bb62a28bbb3e156e4a80df425b337f7d356c9e5b2f48762047c735b.webp)

Move the outside rear-view mirror control button (1) to left, and then adjust it to desired direction through 4-direction adjusting button (2) before adjusting left rearview mirror; then press the right side to adjust right rearview mirror; when the control button stays in the middle, rearview mirrors on both sides will not move.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_f32ef4d1` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

In case of any fault to the electrical regulator, press on the edge of surface with fingers to adjust outside rear-view mirror on both sides;
Improper maintenance of rearview mirror will cause damage; do not shave off ice on the surface of rearview mirror. In case rearview mirror is limited due to ice or other objects, never adjust it by force.

Heating of outside rear-view mirror:

Turn on ignition switch, turn air-conditioner knob to windshield defrost mode to start heating of outside rear-view mirror sheet and get rid of rain, mist and moisture on outside rear-view mirror sheet.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_0c1b562b` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When the outside rear-view mirror is unfolded after heating, do not touch outside rear-view mirror surface with hand; Folding of outside rear-view mirror:

![](images/f1fa788a012eaeb00a28eb6214b3183ec29847bbda7ac549604e2d4a4d322408.webp)

Push outside rear-view mirror backward against the vehicle to fold and put it flatly on the body side.

For models with electric folding outside rear-view mirror, press folding control button (3) for electric folding and unfolding of outside rear-view mirror.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_d271318c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

To operate fold switch to fold rearview mirror to the middle until full close position (back extreme position), a light noise will be heard during movement, which is a normal condition. To decline rearview mirror forward excessively will cause damage.

Do not drive with outside rear-view mirror folded, or it will reduce your view and causes collision accidents.
To fold back outside rear-view mirror, do not leave your finger between rearview mirror and its base— danger of injury！
For electric folding featured outside rear-view mirror, the following instructions will apply: in the event that rearview mirror moves due to external force (e.g. Collided in the course of meeting with other cars), it is necessary to fold rearview mirror completely in electric manner. Do not restore rearview mirror by hand, or the rearview mirror functions may be compromised. In case of any fault in electric adjusting and folding of outside rear-view mirror, please contact your JAC dealer immediately.

### Inside rear-view mirror
`jac_s5_en_39c40963` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The inside rear-view mirror consists of manual dimming and auto-dimming rearview mirrors while auto -dimming can be turned on or off through button 2; With ignition switch On, sensor 3 will adjust glass reflectivity based on the darkness for dimming; with reverse gear engaged, dimming function turns off automatically.

Notes:

In case the light to sensor is compromised and shielded by something like sunshade, the auto-dimming inside rear-view mirror will fail or fail to work soundly.

### 1. Remove
`jac_s5_en_faa87db0` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

### 2. Installation
`jac_s5_en_cd1d0022` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Press inside rear-view mirror into its support and make sure it has been well assembled.
2) Mount the inside rear-view mirror support.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_4e304078` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Inside rear-view mirror is connected onto its support. The inside rear-view mirror support is bonded on windshield with plastic polyvinyl butyral glue. To install an independent inside rear-view mirror support or a new part, please prepare the following items:
Loctite™ instant adhesive 312.
Original inside rear-view mirror support (Prepared according to Step 4 and 5 in assembly procedures), or replace with a new one.
Wax marker or crayon.
Isopropyl alcohol.
Cleaning towel.
Fine emery cloth or sandpaper (320 or 360)

① Measure the dimension from top of windshield (1) to top of inside rear-view mirror support (2).

![](images/e279162b22d4c67f47507614b0a13566b2cc0e732056ca7edac7a89fbb70c790.webp)
Schematic diagram for installing inside rear-view mirror support

② Make marks on the outer part of windshield with wax pen or pencil, draw a circle on the external surface of windshield (3) along the inside rear-view mirror support washer.

③ Clean and clear the great circle on the surface of windshield with paper towel soaked with general glass cleaner, general part number or polishing compound, then dry it and clean again with paper towel soaked in isopropyl alcohol, clear cleaning powder or scrubbing solution mark.

### Notes:
`jac_s5_en_d699b9b3` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

To use original inside rear-view mirror support again, remove all marks of factory installed adhesive glue before reassembly.
④ Use a piece of fine (320 or 360) emery cloth or sandpaper to polish inside rear-view mirror or bonding surface of original inside rear-view mirror support.
⑤ Wipe out the polished inside rear-view mirror support with clean paper towel soaked in isopropyl alcohol and dry it naturally before recoating of glue.
⑥ Before assembly on the windshield, prepare inside rear-view mirror support as instructed by the manufacturer.
⑦ Place inside rear-view mirror on pre-marked position properly, not leaving the half round hold end pointing upward; Press inside rear-view mirror support against windshield for 30 \~ 60s and apply constant stress on the windshield.
⑧ 5 minutes later, clear excessive adhesive-glue with paper towel soaked in isopropyl alcohol or window cleaning solution.

### Horn Troubleshooting Checklist
`jac_s5_en_a2c39047` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Step Measure Normal performance Abnormal performance Press horn button, then the horn sounds out; release Horn fails to work or give Press and unscrew horn button horn button, then the horn stops sounding. proper sound

### Troubleshooting schedule for horn fault
`jac_s5_en_ac73e7ca` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:electrical | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Step Measure Yes No
1 2 3 Check if the fuse in indoor fuse box FS36 has been blown down Check horn relay terminals & 4 for proper power supply To step 7 To step 3 To step 2 To step 8 Ground wire short circuit of Relay pins 1 & 2 , check horn for soundness To step 4 To step 9 Check the circuit from relay pin 3 to clock spring M12, pin 12, for intactness 4 To step 5 To step 13 and completeness 5 6 7 Check the spiral coil on clock spring for intactness and soundness To step 6 To step 14 Check wire connection of steering wheel for intactness and soundness To step 16 After removal of faults caused by other circuits, replace the fuse 8 Repair the circuit from FS36 to relay, pins & 4 9 Check the circuit from relay, pin 2 to horn, pin 2 is well established To step 10 To step 16
10 Check the circuit from horn, pin 1 to G04 for intactness and completeness To step 11 To step 12
11 Replace horn
12 Repair the circuit from horn, pin to G04
13 Repair the circuit between relay pin 3 and clock spring M12 pin 12 1 Replace clock spring
15 Repair circuits for steering wheel
16 Repair the circuit between relay, pin 2 and horn, pin 2

### 2. Horn sound distortion
`jac_s5_en_c2c3d388` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Operate horn to confirm any abnormality of horn.
2) In case of any sound distortion or noise in horn, make checks in procedures shown below:
① Check the terminal to see if there is poor contact; If yes, repair it.

② Check the grounding wire connection. In case of obvious poor contact, repair it.

③ Make sure the horn assembly is fastened properly with tightening bolts.

④ Make sure the horn assembly is not in contact with any other object; In case of any contact, reset its position, or bend horn assembly support if necessary.

3) Operate horn to confirm if the fault is still lasting. If yes, determine the tone type of horn:

Low tone

Sharp & weak tone

① When the tone is apparently low, there must be excessive current and check the circuit; If the circuit is under sound condition, then replace horn assembly.

② In the case of obvious sharp & weak tone, the horn may have foreign materials adhered inside, then remove horn assembly and check it for foreign materials.

③ Get rid of any foreign materials adhered and reassemble the horn assembly.

④ In case no foreign material is found adhered or it is impossible to get rid of foreign materials adhered, then replace the horn assembly.

4) Operate the horn to confirm items concerned have been repaired.

### CM-Combination Meter
`jac_s5_en_689bb04f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Functional Specification

![](images/aa57eac4a19c2782ef012143146791903641acfa93f549148ed1ccc13214184e.webp)
Combination Meter Composition
1. Tachometer 2.Speedometer 3.Fuel gauge 4.Water-thermometer

5. Driver information display screen 6. Alarm light and indicator light7. Trip button

### Combination meter can give the following indications:
`jac_s5_en_bb464952` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Speedometer: the speedometer indicates travel speed of the vehicle with the pointer moving based on CAN signals from ESC.
2. Tachometer: the tachometer indicates revolution of engine with the pointer moving based on CAN revolution signals from engine EMS.
3. Water-thermometer: the water-thermometer indicates engine coolant temperature with the pointer moving based on CAN signals from EMS.
4. Fuel gauge: fuel gauge indicates fuel volume left in the fuel tank with the pointer moving based on resistance signal from the fuel sensor.
5. LCD display: mileage accrual mileage subtotal, instantaneous fuel consumption, average fuel consumption, continuous mileage, gear display, average speed, travel time, water-thermometer, fuel gauge, door opening (5 doors).

![](images/616b417a971f63d78a5818e1f69a54029e1ab6a7586497a14387524d9882c8ab.webp)

7. Driver information display screen

![](images/6b1d34c5ed214c94e79a3f4edf2b3c9ebd19f5e90c4c9b688d17d0daf36628eb.webp)

Travel time ,Average speed , Remainder range

Average speed, instantaneous fuel consumption

Subtotal mileage, total mileage

1) Display of the first line: continuous mileage or average speed or Travel time.

Continuous mileage, average speed: update every 10s;

Travel time: counting from the moment when the speed is over 0, maximum 99 hours and 59 minutes.

2) Display of the second line: Instantaneous fuel consumption or average fuel consumption. Instantaneous fuel consumption: when the ignition switch stays at a speed <3km/h, LCD displays ―--L/100km‖, during travelling (speed ≥ 3km/h), the combination meter will make calculation and gives display, LCD will refresh instantaneous fuel consumption at an interval of 2s;

Average fuel consumption: when the ignition switch stays under ignition on (engine not started) condition, LCD displays the last average fuel consumption record; when the engine is started, if ECU gives no fuel consumption signal, LCD will display the last average fuel consumption record; with fuel consumption signal, it will display the value of average fuel consumption; LCD display refreshes every 10s.

### Combination meter can give the following indications:
`jac_s5_en_7f204d4a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:fuel_ev | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3) Display of the third line: Total mileage or Mileage Subtotal.

Mileage subtotal display range: 0km〜999. 9km, precision: 0.lkm; total mileage display range: 0〜 999999km, precision: l km.

8. Trip button

Button operation: turn it rightward from the first to the third line (from Up to Down) and leftward from the third to the first line to select a line; when a line is selected, flashing will be seen to indicate the one selected; the press 0. 3s\~2s to switch display of the line, press and hold >2s to clear travel time, average speed, average fuel consumption and mileage subtotal; Press and hold ≥2s and keep the ignition switch at ON position while the engine is not started to refresh continuous mileage.

9. Alarm light and indicator light
1) Charging/discharging indicator light

When the ignition switch is at “白 ， position, charging/discharging indicator light turns on and then goes off after the engine is started. In case the indicator light turns on when the engine is running, then the charging system must have stops charging the battery. At this moment, turn off all unnecessary power consuming accessories immediately, e.g. radio, air-conditioner and internal lights; in the mean time, take special care not to switch off the car since restarting the engine will causes faster power consumption of the battery. Please contact JAC authorized service center to check the charging system as soon as possible.

2) Engine oil pressure alarm light

When engine oil is under pressure or the system is in fault then the engine oil pressure alarm light will turn on. Under normal condition, the engine oil pressure alarm light turns on when the ignition switch turns to ―ON‖ position and then goes off after the engine is started. In case this alarm light stays on after the engine is started or turns on during traveling, then there must have insufficient engine oil or some other faults occur. In this case, for safety reason, pull over and stop the engine, then wait for a few minutes to check engine oil volume. In case of insufficient oil, refill some engine oil to restart the engine. In case the alarm light fails to turn off within 10s, then shut down the engine. Please contact JAC authorized service center to check the system.

Note: to run the engine runs with engine oil pressure alarm light on may causes damage to the engine immediately!

3) Electric power steering system fault alarm light

When the electric power steering system is in fault, the electric power steering system fault alarm light will turn on, provided the engine is started.

Note: To plug in and out connector at the interface of EPS control, please disconnect power supply first in case of damage to the control.

4) Engine fault indicator light

This indicator light (only for gas engine ) is part of the internal diagnostic system, used to monitor engine fault. When the ignition switch turns to ―ON‖ position, the indicator light turns on and then off after the engine is started. In case the indicator light turns on during traveling or fails to turn on when at ―ON‖ position, please contact JAC authorized service center for check as soon as possible.

### Combination meter can give the following indications:
`jac_s5_en_6a443004` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:engine | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

5) High beam light indicator light

This indicator light will go on when the high beam light turns on or warning signal is given by flashing high beam light lamp.

6) Front fog lamp indicator light

When the front fog lamp is turned on, indicator light goes on.

7) Rear fog lamp indicator light

When the rear fog lamp is turned on, indicator light goes on.

8) Direction indicator light

When driveway change or turn signal is given, direction indicator lights on left or right side of instrument panel will flash. If this lamp fails to flash, flash at a fast frequency or fail to work completely, then the steering signal system must have faults and please contact JAC authorized service center for repair as soon as possible, or other driver will never notice your signal. Press hazard warning lamp button, the two direction indicator lights will flash and all turn signal lights outside will flash too.

9) Hand brake and braking system fault indicator light

When the ignition key turns to ―ON‖ position or ―START‖ position , this indicator light turns on and then off when the service brake is released after the engine is started. In case the indicator light turns on at other time, then slow down and drive the car to a safe position to park.

When the fuel is insufficient in the master brake cylinder, the indicator light turns on. It will turn off after enough braking fluid complaint with D0T4 standard is filled while no other faults are detected, then drive the car carefully to JAC authorized service center for check. When the indicator light fails to turn off and other faults are detected, then do not drive the car, but deliver the car to JAC authorized service center for service with dedicated trailer or in other safe ways.

Warning: when the braking system fault indicator light turns on, do not drive the car any more, as it indicates there‘s something wrong with the braking system. It is dangerous to drive the car when faults happen to the braking system; it may causes collision accident and even personal injury.

10) Seat belt indicator light

When the ignition key turns from ―OFF‘ to ―ON‖ position or ―START‖ position, the seat belt indicator light turns on and fasten your seat belt after stating the engine, then the indicator light goes off.

11) Air bag indicator light

When the ignition switch stays at ‗ON‖ position or after engine starts, air bag (SRS) indicator light turns on and flashes continuously for 6s before going off, which indicates air bag system is ready. When the ignition switch is at ―ON‖ position or after engine starts, in the event that SRS indicator light is not on, stays on after flashing for 6s, turns on during traveling or turns on and off now and then, it indicates SRS is not under sound condition, please contact JAC authorized service center to check the system.

Warning: Ignoring the on/off phenomenon of air bag indicator light may cause serious casualty accidents in case airbag system has fault.

12) ABS fault alarm light

When the ignition switch turns to ―ON position, ABS fault alarm light turns on and stays on for seconds before going off, indicating completion of ABS system self-check and it is under sound working condition. When the alarm light stays on or turns on during travelling, or fails to turn on when the ignition switch turns to ―ON‖ position, the ABS system must have faults. At this time, the general braking system will work, but ABS braking system fails, please contact JAC authorized service center to check the vehicle as soon as possible.

### Combination meter can give the following indications:
`jac_s5_en_7e57ac42` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:brakes | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Warning: when ABS alarm light stays on or turns on during travelling, ABS may have faults. At the time, wheels will be locked faster when braking and it may leads to vehicle drifting.

13) Coolant temperature warning indicator light

When the coolant temperature warning indicator light turns on, it indicates engine coolant temperature is above normal value, the pull over, stop the engine, open engine hood and check coolant level and pump drive belt. In case of any fault in the cooling system, please contact JAC authorized service center to check the system.

14) EOBD indicator light

This indicator light is part of the internal diagnostic system used to monitor exhaust emission. In case any part in connection with exhaust system or the exhaust system fails resulting improper exhaust adjustment, then this fault indicator light will turn on. Turn ignition switch to ―ON‖ position, then this indicator light will turn on and it goes off after engine starting, In the event that this indicator light turns on or flashes during traveling, or fails at ―ON‖ position, then the emission control system must have unsteady operating conditions and excessive exhaust emission. At this moment, the car still can move, but please contact JAC authorized service center to check it as soon as possible, because continuing traveling for long time may cause further damage to parts in connection with exhaust emission control and worse emission.

15) Fuel alarm indicator light

When onl y 7L fuel is left in the tank, then fuel alarm indicator light turns on and refills the tank as soon as possible. When fuel alarm indicator light is on or fuel level is lower than ―E‖ position, it will cause engine kill and damage to catalytic converter.

16) TCU fault indicator light (if equipped)

TCU fault indicator light is only equipped on DCT transmission based vehicle. In case of AMT transmission fault, this indicator light will turn on. In the event that TCU fault indicator light turns on during traveling, please contact JAC authorized service center immediately to check the system.

17) ESP indicator light

In the case of ESP system shielding (switch off), ESP OFF indicator light stays on; when ESP indicateosr EliSgPhtfaiuslton an,dplfelasheicnog,ntaEcStPJAwCoarkutshosroiuznedlsye;rvwichencenEtSerP aind ichaetcork tlhigehstysteayms. on, it

18) HDC indicator light

Press the button to start HDC function the indicator light turns on and Green; press the button again to stop HDC function, then the indicator light goes off. When HDC is functioning, the green light flashes; in case of HDC fault, HDC alarm indicator light will turn on and Red, please contact JAC authorized service center to check the system.

19) Alarm light for abnormal tire pressure

This indicator light stays on indicating high or low pressure and high temperature alarm , please contact JAC authorized service center to check tires.

20) Tire Pressure Monitoring System fault alarm indicator light
This indicator light is on indicating system fault TPMS and sensor with lower power, please contact JAC authorized service center to check the system.

### Precautions:
`jac_s5_en_253bb0d4` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

It is only allowed to use with 12V D.C. negative ground wire system.

Disconnect negative pole of battery of the vehicle before installation and connection.

To replace fuse, make sure the fuse of the same specifications is used. When a fuse of different specifications is used, it will cause serious damage to the machine.

Make sure no external objects like sharp pin enter the machine, or it will cause fault or even endanger the equipment.

If your vehicle is going to stay in an overcooling or overheated environment for long time, then do not operate until after it has come back to room temperature inside the vehicle.

Turn the volume to the proper position when external alarm can be heard (e.g. hoot).

### Before operation
`jac_s5_en_c2120ad5` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Do not use too high volume because it will hinder external sound and causes traffic accidents.

To conduct complicated operation, park the car first.

Do not open the housing for repair without permission, please contact your dealer or experienced technicians first.

### System overview
`jac_s5_en_574fe0b8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The parking assist system consists of sensor, BCM control module and alarm device. It is to determine if there‘s any obstruction on the reversing track based on ultrasonic ranging theory and computer data fuzzy processing technology and indicate the distance of the nearest obstruction. Put the lever into reversing gear, the reverse switch and reversing lamp turn on, then the controller receives reverse signal and analyzes return signals transferred accurately and completely through control of ultrasonic signals from ultrasonic sensor based on ―self transmission, self reception‖ and ―multiple transmission, single reception‖ principle of the sensor, to determine obstruction within sensing range and distance of the nearest obstruction, furthermore, establishes communication with the buzzer which will buzz at different frequency based on the range to indicate obstruction distance for safety purpose.

When the reversing gear is engaged, the system starts to work and give self-check, at this time, if the buzzer gives a beep; the system is functioning soundly; if the buzzer gives two bees, the system is in fault. Self-check results are shown below:

![](images/f0623114935c82dbddbf5a7456a39eccb25be8a5fbf0c280ecb4dd3cb80e9cc6.webp)

Reversing sensor enters the sensing range as indicated below and sends different frequency of pulse signal to the buzzer according to the ranging of obstructions detected, and then the buzzer will sound at different frequency accordingly, as shown in the following table:

![](images/4b4694ac8daa30c2807071766e9f5e11c6e7586234e78ea34a6f4768c350361f.webp)

Obstruction distance Warning tone Area 0. 90m〜1. 50 m Once every 1/2s Early warning area 0. 60m〜0. 90m Once every 1/4s Warning area 0. 35m〜0. 60m Once every 1/8s Guard area 0〜0. 35m All the time Danger area

In reversing, the parking sensor detects obstructions within the sensing range and starts distance based alarm at the same time. As the vehicle moves closer to the obstruction, pulse alarm will sound at a smaller interval. When <0.35m away, the alarm turns into continuous sound and no more reversing from this moment.

### Note:
`jac_s5_en_42e0aa55` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■ the sensor has dead zone where it can not detect the obstruction. Please pay special caution to children and pets, because the sensor can not detect them under all conditions, or it is dangerous to cause accidents!

■The Parking Assist System can not substitute the driver to make judgment of external conditions. The driver shall concentrate in reverse or similar operation. Please always keep an eye on the environment around the vehicle.

■As the reverse goes warned low obstructions may seem to have gone in the sensing range and no more alarms will be sounded. The system can not detect certain objects sometimes, e.g. chain, trailer drawbar, small painted vertical bar or fence, if not take care, there will be risk of accidents.

`jac_s5_en_4bd02f93` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■To install sensor assembly, prevent the sensor core being stressed, in case of damage to internal parts; first, put the sensor through bumper pore, then fit the adjustment panel on the inner wall of bumper, press down the lock and turn 90° to lock it. ### 1. System Fault
`jac_s5_en_0d37d455` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Phenomenon: under reversing condition, the buzzer gives no alert sound.

Fault cause: the Parking Assist System fails, usually due to inactivation of working power supply;
when the battery supplies power properly, then it must be the power wire under poor contact.

Maintaining Method: Check ground wire continuity between I/P-G terminal 29 and terminal of probe R18 (battery voltage, this power goes the same circuit with that of reversing lamp)， as well as probe terminal 2. Pin 46 of BCM connector M23 is for LIN signal, check if it is well connected to terminal 3. In case of nothing wrong in the aforesaid checks, then replace left probe or BCM.

### 2. Sensor fault
`jac_s5_en_b13ff0f3` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Phenomenon: under reversing condition, a certain sensor always fails to detect obstructions.

Fault cause: the Parking Assist System is working, but one sensor fails.

Maintaining Method: under reversing condition, press your ear against the sensor and listen carefully to see if there‘s light tick (compare to sound sensor); if no noise occurs, the sensor power supply must be under sound condition, then check the communications line between sensor and BCM for proper connection; if the communication line is connected properly, then replace the sensor.

### 3. Low sensing capability of sensor
`jac_s5_en_9c782496` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Phenomenon: under reversing condition, a certain sensor always shows poor sensing capacity.

Fault cause: the Parking Assist System works well, but a sensor has low sensing performance.

Maintaining method: clean foreign materials on the sensor surface.

### Note:
`jac_s5_en_0adbe136` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■To reverse on a rough road, the system may send alarms occasionally and it is normal.
■To reverse in a narrow alley, the system may send alarms occasionally and is normal.

### Composition of Wiper Washing System
`jac_s5_en_d8ff4b99` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The windshield shall be cleaned and cleared of rain, dust and dirt timely, or it will compromise driving safety; for this reason, wiper washing system is designed and provided. This model is designed with windshield wiper of 5 control levels: high and low speed, intermittent control, interval adjustable and washing control, consisting of drive motor, worm and gear as well as rocker arm, blade, etc.; the washer system is composed of cleanser tank, motor, pipeline and nozzle, etc.

### Inching wiping:
`jac_s5_en_2ed2b6ea` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Move up combination switch handle to ―MIST‖ position, then the wiper starts working; when the external force is eliminated, the combination switch handle returns and the wiper stops.

②Intermittent wiping:

Move down combination switch handle to ―INT‖ position, turn the knob on the handle to adjust interval of wiper movement, as shown in the figure.

Model equipped with rain sensor is ―INT‖ gear, activate rain sensor, at the time, turn knob to adjust rain sensor sensitivity.

![](images/6aa9f312074e1bb01f6aab5b48111223b872a0dd46c6dc7dfcefbbc8d172edff.webp)

③Slow wiping: move down combination switch to ―L0‖, the wiper starts wiping slowly. ④Fast wiping: move down combination switch to ―HI‖, the wiper starts wiping fast. ⑤Turn off front windshield wiper: move combination switch handle to ―OFF‖ and the wiper stops then.

![](images/556f5dac31f1c947299cfecdafe9a9361bc5ca5948347ab0c9d99981b036a0b8.webp)

### Rear windshield wiper:
`jac_s5_en_a87624e1` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Intermittent wiping: turn the end control knob tothe slot ―INT‖.

Turn on rear windshield wiper: turn the end control knob to the slot ―ON‖.

Turn off rear windshield wiper: move control lever to the basic position ―OFF‖.

Windshield washer:

Front window sprinkling and cleaning: pull wiper control lever inward.

Rear window sprinkling and cleaning: pull wiper control lever outward.

![](images/c899fefba292fab5966c30c4d3366b004c8e35e292d905ccae70c979942c7ed4.webp)

### Schematic Diagram of Wiper Washing System
`jac_s5_en_a9ab17f1` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/333bf9926064833d3d35d97a68e41e8d2f1823d8d6d154c069dd7d184b1999e6.webp)

Notes:

■To remove connector wires for electrical appliances in the system remove the negative pole of battery first and wait for minute, or it may cause personal injury.

■To disassemble and assemble components, wrap the part to remove with clothing to protect from damage.

■To remove metal clamp from trimming, cover the edge of slotted screwdriver with clothing.

■Take care not damage body elements.

■To install body trimmings make sure the clamp is placed securely within the hole on body panel and then carefully press them in first.

In case it is impossible for you to work alone on certain component removal, find a partner to

work together in case of falling.

Do not apply too much force in disassembling certain trimmings because it may lead to deformation or damage of parts.

### Front Windshield Wiper
`jac_s5_en_fd8fe78c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Structure: four-linkage mechanism used, available with pedestrian protection and motor thermal cut-out protection features, fixed at three points and boneless blade used.

Function: Combination Switch handle control, available with high and low speed, intermittent control, interval adjustable and washer control options.

### 2. Windshield Wiper Arm Pressure Inspection
`jac_s5_en_27233bb2` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Make windshield wiper arm and blade move to the middle position.

2) Remove the wiper blade from windshield wiper arm.

3) Connect the spring scale to one end of the windshield wiper arm and measure perpendicularity to the windshield, pull up the windshield wiper arm to typical operating height (the height when wiper blade is installed) at the torque 6〜8N·m.

4) In case the measurement is not consistent, then replace windshield wiper arm.

5) Assemble the wiper blade onto the windshield wiper arm.

### 3. Check and Adjust the Rubber Strip for Wiper Blade
`jac_s5_en_b36a9503` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Remove the wiper blade from windshield wiper arm.

2) Check the length of rubber strip for windshield wiper blade.

3) If the rubber part in contact with glass is not staying +/-15°away from the center line of windshield wiper blade, then replace the rubber strip for windshield wiper blade.

4) Assemble the wiper blade onto the windshield wiper arm.

### 4. Front Windshield Wiper Switch Inspection
`jac_s5_en_fb4e8604` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When wiper washing system switch works, check the continuity between terminals. In case of any fault, replace wiper washing system and turn on front windshield wiper switch.

![](images/3433bd98a3dc28d8c80365c08a2c0119a0827a034910f59c1d006331994bfdbb.webp)

`jac_s5_en_07412a6e` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Use the control lever to operate the motor at low speed.
2) Detach terminal 3 for motor connector to stop it at any position other than OFF.
3) Connect terminals 3 and 2.
4) Connect the battery to terminals 1 and 4 at positive (+) and negative (-) end pole respectively.
5) Check if the motor stays at ―OFF‖ position.

### 2. Wiper Washing Motor Inspection
`jac_s5_en_4d558a4f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Connect washing motor to the water tank and fill the water tank.

2) Case 1: connect battery (+) pole and (-) pole guide wire to terminal and 2 respectively; Case 2: connect battery (+) pole and (-) pole guide wire to terminal and 3.

3) In both cases, check if the motor is under sound condition and if the washing motor is working.
In case 1, check if there‘s water spraying from front nozzle while from rear nozzle in case 2.

4) In case of any abnormality, replace the washing motor.

Note: To add water into reservoir tank, check the filter for any foreign materials or pollution first.
Clean the filter as required.

![](images/a09f13e171bbe95db22cceb5f54ac557f50a563ca68c93aba9079cdaddac33d7.webp)

### 3. Disassemble wiper washer:
`jac_s5_en_c0686009` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1) Remove the negative pole of battery.

2) Remove the front bumper.

3)Remove front (A) and rear (B) washing hose, and then detach washing motor connector (C).

4) Disassemble the washing motor.

![](images/efb1cd6bb36828ab6876807900e6b6194b2b5784984bb5ed6ed05f098210867b.webp)

5) Screw off the 2 bolts and 1 nut, then disassemble washer.

### Note:
`jac_s5_en_a464b9e9` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■To repair the washing pump; take care not to damage its seals.

■Do not operate the washing pump before refilling its reservoir tank, or it may cause early fault due to disoperation.

### 4. Disassemble wiper washing system switch
`jac_s5_en_ad1df572` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:lighting | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1)Disconnect the negative pole of battery terminal.

2)Screw off the three bolts and then disassemble upper and lower covers for steering column.

3)Detach lighting switch connector and wiper switch connector.

4)Press down lock pin and disassemble wiper switch.

![](images/2c0277db821292872a24f6f11e3a68a6f48b62002cba87e9004a5c628124e461.webp)

Note:

■To disassemble wiper washer switch only, release wiper switch lock with steering wheel disassembled.

■Take care not to damage and scratch the protective cover and related components.

■To disassemble upper and lower covers; take care not to damage latch hook.

### Ck -Ignition switch
`jac_s5_en_a5fc9398` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Ignition switch location diagram Ignition switch sits on the right of steering column under the steering wheel.

![](images/9812b2cae18cbc428bbbc007722e2c0d4b42409c38483c628fb5ca49e4ae2bc0.webp)
Ignition switch location diagram

Functional description of ignition switch:

Ignition switch is available with 4 gears, which are:

### LOCK
`jac_s5_en_3070efca` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

The key can be inserted or pulled out only in LOCK position. For parking lock, turn the key counterclockwise to plug out the key from ―LOCK‖ position, then turn the steering wheel until engagement sound of lock pin is heard, when the steering wheel will be locked to prevent the vehicle being stolen.

### ACC
`jac_s5_en_eca4e09e` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

With key at this position, you can use the audio system and cigar lighter. It is not recommended to operate the said electrical accessories when the engine is started in order to prevent battery power loss.

### ON
`jac_s5_en_ae973282` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Turn the key to this position, corresponding indicator light on the instrument panel will be on for inspection. At the same time, all installations and components on the car are ready to start. The key will stay at this position during travelling.

Do not leave the key at "ON" position for long time when the engine is not started, because it will cause self-discharge and thus battery power loss.

### START
`jac_s5_en_48479d2c` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:transmission | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

This position is only used for engine ignition. Turn the key to this position to start the engine will start and release it after engine starting, and then it will automatically go back to the "ON" position.

Sometimes you may find that, after inserting the key, you can not turn it from the "LOCK" position, this may be because you start steering wheel locking device, then just turn the steering wheel slightly to relieve it.

Start the Engine

Before the start, for a car with manual transmission, the joystick should be placed in the neutral position, while you depress the clutch pedal.

Release the ignition key right after the engine starts; turn it back to "ON" position from "START".
Never let the starter and the engine running at the same time.

After start, the chilled engine may make a loud running noise for short time, because it has to settle

oil pressure first during adjustment of hydraulic valve clearance, which is normal condition.

Notes

Avoid to run the engine for long time during parking, drive the vehicle to move as soon as possible.

Avoid high speed running and acutely acceleration before the engine reaches its operating temperature.

■ For vehicle with exhaust catalytic converter, do not start it by tracing for over 50 meters, otherwise the unburned fuel may penetrate into catalytic converter and damage it.

■To start through traction, try to use the battery of other vehicles for auxiliary starting first.

### Hood assembly diagram
`jac_s5_en_64d8a660` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1-hood hook lock 2- heat -insulation pad of hood 3-Hood fastener 4- Engine compartment sealing strip 5-Hood rubber strip 6-hood assembly 7-hood hinge 8-hood buffer block 9-hood lock body

Heat-insulation pad of hood replacement 1)Removal ① Open the hood.

②Remove the 14 fastener on heat-insulation pad of the hood.

A二

Remove heat-insulation pad of the hood.

2) Installation Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly. Notes: hood strut bar replacement: ■In removal or installing hood strut bar, additional support must be used, so as to avoid vehicle damage or person injury.

1)Disassembly

Open the hood and support it

Remove engine compartment sealing strip.

③Remove the 2 bolts on the strut bar.

④ Remove the strut bar. ■Tightening torque:9〜11 N·m

![](images/853cfa9365b7d5499d9d9e441faa26b6864817868a3faff53713b66a9249fb2f.webp)

2) Installation procedures:
Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

Hood adjustment

1) Longitudinal and transverse gap adjustment
① Remove hood lock， release the nut of hood hinge and close hood。
② Adjust longitudinal and transverse gap, open the hood， fix the bolts and tighten them to specified torque.
③ Install hood lock body temporarily and move it transversely, so as to make the hood latch hole and the lock aligned, until the latch hole and center of the lock are upright in the view from the front.
④ Tighten hood lock and fix the bolts to specified torque.
2) Front height adjustment
①Remove hood lock body, rotate hood buffer block on beam of engine compartment, to adjust the height, until the hood is lower than the front fender to 1-1.5 mm.
② Tighten hood lock temporarily, and confirm where the hood lock body is by locking the latch bolt. Make sure if hood lock and latch bolt are loose, then tighten the bolts of hood lock to specified torque。

Tightening torque:9〜11 N•m

### 3) Adjustment steps
`jac_s5_en_b9fc3bad` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

①Open the hood.

② Release the bolt between the hood and the hinge.

■Tinghtening torque:25N•m

③Adjust the position of the hood and the front fender.

④Close the hood and check if it is aligned.

⑤Adjust the hood buffer block to an appropriate location according to the surface gap of the hood.

⑥Adjust the hood lock body until the lock hook and lock body meshing well.

Tighten the bolt of the hood hinge. Close the hood.

### Fault diagnosis:
`jac_s5_en_ba2db8df` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Elements under the hood or on the engine compartment may be loose or interfering, that‘s why some interior noises occur, then the noises are transmitted to the passenger compartment...

### The reasons why the noises under the hood are transmitted are including:
`jac_s5_en_2db2fbe8` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Elements on the engine compartment are loose or interfering.
2. Elements across the engine compartment are loose or interfering.
3. Engine compartment bracket and the connector are loosed.
4. Radiator fixing pins are loosed.
5. Hood bumper is misaligning.
6. Hood lock is misaligning. These noises can be transmitted to the cabin and hard to close off. The best way is to fix, adjust, move or segregate only one element each time, then test the noise reduction effect in moving, and investigate the elements one by one to find the epicenter.

### Fender
`jac_s5_en_c6092f3f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Removal
1) Remove the front bumper;

2) Remove the fixing plate of the front bumper;

3) Remove the engine compartment sealing plate

![](images/19126ba4e872336990d93f23a2c8f21dde8de8aabe81737d6900a8a885efd22f.webp)

4) Remove the strips in the front samller fender side;

5) Remove the triangle trim plate of the fender;

6) Remove the forepart of the skirt trim plate;

7) Remove the front wheel cowling;

![](images/2e817eeb15347334dbc76d8bf20010d275625d836c7b3289522ff2f4680740c6.webp)

8) Remove the front door;

9) Remove the acoustic baffle of the fender

10) Remove the 8 fixing screws on the fender;
11) Remove the fender.

![](images/d002dcc3b8020b3c95e85f71c08e1044edda4a2b2102542c0b687be23683388c.webp)

2. Installation Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

### Note:
`jac_s5_en_d7a94964` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■After installation, check if the fender is in place and if the gap with the body is appropriate. If not, adjust or re-install them.
■The replacement of the left and right front fender follow the same steps.

### Front door
`jac_s5_en_e648f943` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/cdac2f50de0ab2995a61ffa7e6cd09ad3822bf3bd282943fbfd75abf01d0b308.webp)

Lock assembly diagram of left front door
![](images/55867c75b88a8524f19cd3f3d287cc11567215a0ab7af1afd4f9fe059863b89d.webp)

### Note:
`jac_s5_en_bdc3990a` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■The other structure of right front door lock assembly is symmetrical to left front door‘s, except for no lock cylinder. The disassembly method is the same with left front door‘s.

■When removing and installing the door assembly, do a good job in protection, so as not to scratch the door and the body of the vechicle.

■When door assembly installation is completed, you must do assembly adjustment to make sure that the doors can normally open / close, while you should also paint the head of the hinge mounting bolts (body color).

■Check if the rotating parts of the hinge is in poor lubricating. If necessary, apply ―grease‖.

Due to heavy door components, many people are required for coordination in the process of removal.

### Replacement of Front Door:
`jac_s5_en_c904a067` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Disassembly ①Remove the negative pole of the battery, and wait for 1 min at least.

， Remove the fixing screws of door limiter.Tightening torque:1 0 〜 1 2 N • m

![](images/ff2823bb301d7822fe582ab0de793e6d505225a2ddd09c1cc75ae0e025083ddc.webp)

Remove the fixing screws of door assembly. tightening torque:25 N•m
Note:
■The removal should be divided into several
times. It is forbidden to loosen everything once.

The removal should be from Down to Up. ④Pull out the door wire harness rubber sleeve, disconnect the door electrical connector. ⑤Put up the door with assistance and put it down in an appropriate place.

2. Installation Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

### Note:
`jac_s5_en_d5839558` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Note:

■The element must be installed according to the original position.

■After installation, check if the door switch work well and if the door lock up well. If not, it should be re-installed or adjusted.

Front door fastener replacement:

Removal

① Mark the present position of the fastener;

② Remove the fixing screws of the fastener;

Remove the door fastener.

2) InstallationAssemble it in reverse sequence as did indisassembly.。

Front door fastener adjustment:

Release the fastener bolts; ② Move the fastener to the required direction and make sure the fastener is always in the center of the lock.

③ Tighten the fixing screws of the fastener; Close the door and check if the surface and gap meet the requirements.

Door outer handle holder replacement 1. Disassembly 1) Remove the front door fastener;

2)Loosen the 3 bolts in trim plate of open handle;

![](images/aed9314a2f5799cdbd3f855fd1956517417f0cd4123b221ed0e2a9f5887cd8dc.webp)

![](images/2adc33eb8de1bad3a87fb070bff270d394b8162ff66a3d7d6773670ca9d31855.webp)

3) Remove the trim plate;
4) Loosen 1 fixing screw of outer handle;

5) Remove the outer handle.
2. Installation
Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.
Hint:
The replacement of components on left and right front door is the same.

![](images/76f4df78f094fa834b6480051b79f3108be0c2f3d3a385f1ba6dcdf67705e561.webp)

### Rear door
`jac_s5_en_a75d925e` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/2465968ea4594597cc50db42358efd9b0c5a663bfb2e0bf9c92579f7ab826549.webp)
Rear door lock assembly drawing

![](images/650fc99e7a028d7bb382b358c41c01bb55c81dd4175edfecb2787f0a61ffd229.webp)

Note: The structure of right rear door assembly is symmetrical to the left rear door assembly.

Rear door trim plate replacement: 1. Removal 1) Pry off the triangle cover plate on rear door frame;

2) Pry off the rear door glass lifter switch and disconnect the harness;

3) Remove the switch;

![](images/98eebd54d529a59c53c7298387d07d544793828c4023642e3a6c65d173ed283b.webp)

4) Pry off the outer housing of inner handle;

5) Loosen the 2 tightening bolts on the inner handle;

6) Pry off the inner open switch clip plate and loosen the screw;

7) Loosen 1 fixing screw on inner armrest

![](images/fd656621fd797cf679486b2125e437d68fc09decd1c5fbc402c8764a5b1a9f80.webp)

8) Loosen 1 fixing screw beside the door;

9)Loosen 1 fixing screw in the lower of the door;
10) Pry off the rear door trim plate; Notes:

Disassemble trim plate, mind the fastener. Otherwise the trim plate is easily damaged. 11) Release the fastener on drawing wire of open handle;

12) Remove the rear door interior trim plate.

![](images/250464e460e6204b0a7672382ae1efcedb28c3d04c13f45c49fc32f19e62e037.webp)

2.Installation
Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

Note:

■When installing the rear door interior trim plate, make sure firstly that the fastener fix correctly in the plate hole of car body, and then press them in.

Rear door waterproof membrane replacement:

1) Disassembly
① Remove the rear door interior trim plate;

P

② Remove the 2 fixing brackets of interior trim plate

![](images/91b23b27b459d7c9baaae4d5ff20c0d7fdf5f7f73ef6c336cba380b3059eaacb.webp)

③ Rip off the waterproof membrane and get it down.

![](images/55d00d32a6c86c56b2e1a2353cf12f8085ada7a05f6fd968887e3ef1496537bb.webp)

2) Installation
Assemble it in reverse sequence as did in disassembly.

Note:

In installation of waterproof membrane, do not cover the fastener hole. ■Do not touch the adhesive surface so as not to weaken the effect of another bonding. Replacement of rear windshield sealing strip: 1) Disassembly ① Remove the 2 fixing screws on rear door outside glass sealing strip;

② Remove rear door outside window weather strip.

![](images/cd139588774972cc4ccfd837bc3b23a7d45fbb4d62db078d37f9374a39e9bbc7.webp)

### Note：
`jac_s5_en_9e665ab7` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Align the outside window weather strip to vehicle body, and install the outside window weather strip from one side slowly.

### Note
`jac_s5_en_a6a12b0d` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

When removing and installing components, one should make protection to avoid components scratch or damage.

■ When removing buckles of interior panel, please pack the slotted screwdriver to avoid scratching the vehicle body and interior panel.

■ When installing vehicle body trimming, ensure buckles to be rightly installed into panel hole in vehicle body, and press them in carefully.

■ When removing and installing bigger components, several persons are need to coordinate in order to avoid components damage and personal injury.

When removing and installing trimming, please don’t overexert to avoid components deformation or damage.

### 1) Removal
`jac_s5_en_d9614615` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

① Open engine cover;

![](images/2ff588b2feacbf0eb94889b6b958953a59cc19faaed38a99a09319d084bc02d2.webp)

② Remove 18 fixed bolts on front bumper;

![](images/4066fd5928d95563d5e261f4acef4dd32a1a9f85dedceb1277ae9d040079f123.webp)

③ Take down front bumper from bumper fixed plate;

![](images/96ce27c15033d79e8a612165998a06e4a149c7238ce52ecde85f80692c2d5bcf.webp)

④ Disconnect left/right front fog light wiring harness connector;
⑤ Take down front bumper.

![](images/7fdbebdda069dcc3d655fd87c7d389cf6faee37ba8cac9a6efd2098582808dd9.webp)

### Note：
`jac_s5_en_b6f1042e` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■ After installing, check whether there are surface difference or clearance between joint surface of bumper and vehicle body;
■ Bumper is resin material, please don’t contact with grease etc. amyctic chemical agent; if contact carelessly, please clean with water immediately;
■ To remove and install the bumper conveniently, place the vehicle on the lift mechanism, and ensure lift mechanism to be locked safely;
■ When removing and installing bumper, two persons are need to avoid drop and damage bumper;
■ When removing and installing bumper, use soft protective firm to protect bumper exterior surface to avoid scratch;
■ When installing front bumper, have to connect the bumper to the bumper fixed bracket.

### 1) Removal
`jac_s5_en_b8900c93` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

③ Take down buffer beam.

![](images/6be6d49bfb14181f203857529334474a417c4ebe780fdf83c0b23b6868cf0ef1.webp)

`jac_s5_en_319196a1` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■ Before installing, check whether rear wheel casing cracks, and miss installing fixed buckle of wheel casing firstly.

Ensure wheel casing fit well with vehicle body and rear bumper after installing.

### Note：
`jac_s5_en_2f071b1f` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

■ Bumper is resin material, please don’t contact with grease etc. amyctic chemical agent; and avoid the overexertion;

When removing and installing, don’t scratch bumper exterior surface.

`jac_s5_en_fc5461f9` | s5 | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual
